    quote: This part contains the specifications for rehabilitation and restoration works (Part 4, Part B) of Section
      H in Volume 2 of BMRCL Tender No. `BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157`, concerning restoration
      works carried out for safety reasons after demolition or dismantling of buildings and structures identified
      for Bangalore Metro Rail Project Phase 3.

- kind: parameter
  text: BMRCL may appoint a consultant or Engineer to conduct Building Condition Studies (BCS) and recommend remediation,
    structural restoration (such as front walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns), and demolition sequences,
    with measurements restricted to buildings completed under such supervision.
  evidence_ids:
  - J4d5b89fdb3b2cdb4
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    quote: "{\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"1.    The BMRCL may appoint\

      \ of demolition including remedial measures\\n             required to be undertaken prior to demolition and/or\
      \ during demolition and/or post\\n             demolition.\\n                    It is anticipated that, certain\
      \ structures will be required to be constructed for safety and\\n              functional reasons as a result\
      \ of Building Condition Study. The structures anticipated are\\n               front walls, doors, windows,\
      \ beams and columns etc.\\n          The BMRCL has identified few items which are to be executed in this contract.\

- kind: summary
  text: Order of precedence for standards and technical specifications establishes that provisions in the Employer
    Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical Specifications take priority over General Technical Specifications, KPWD
    Specifications, MORTH Specifications, and Standard Codes of Practice respectively.
  evidence_ids:
  - Je0c9f938cf2098a9
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    quote: "{\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"2.      All Standards, Technical\
      \ Specifications and Codes of Practice referred to shall be latest\\n             editions including all applicable\
      \ official amendments and revisions. The contractor shall\\n         make available at site all relevant KPWD\
      \ Specifications and Indian Standard Codes of\\n             practice as applicable.\\n              In case\
      \ of discrepancy among Standard codes of practice, Technical Specifications and\\n             provisions\
      \ in Sub clauses in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n              Specifications’,\
      \ the order of precedence will be as below:\\n\\n                               i)  Provision in this ‘Employer\
      \ Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n                    Specifications’\\n                        \
      \         ii)  Technical Specifications\\n                                  iii) KPWD Specifications\\n  \

    in metric sizes permitting nearest FPS units upon Engineer approval without extra cost or rebate.

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"1\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"1.\
      \    MATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP:     The whole of the materials used on the work\\n               shall be\
      \ new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants\\n               shall\
      \ be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best,\\n           most\
      \ workman like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the\\n             Engineer.\\\
      n           The workmanship is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall\\n  \
      \           take all steps immediately to make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must\\n \
      \          be made of a special tradesman in all aspects of the work and allowances must be made\\n      \
      \          in the rates for the same.\\n\\n              Contractor shall maintain uniform quality and consistency\
      \ in workmanship throughout the\\n             execution of the work.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 34,\n      \"text\": \"Materials shall be of the best approved quality obtainable and they shall comply\
      \ with the\\n       respective Indian Standard Specification.\\n        In case of non-availability of materials\
      \ in metric sizes, the nearest size in FPS units shall\\n      be provided with the prior approval of the\
      \ Engineer for which neither extra will be paid\\n       nor shall any rebate be recovered.\\n           \
      \ It shall be obligatory for the Contractor to furnish Certificate, if demanded by the Engineer,\\n      \
      \ from manufacturer or the material supplier that the work has been carried out by using\\n         their\
      \ material and as per their recommendation.\\n       Unless otherwise shown on the drawings or mentioned in\
      \ the schedule of quantities or\\n        special specifications the quality of materials, workmanship, dimensions,\
      \ etc., shall be as\\n        specified here in under.\\n         All equipment and facilities for carrying\
      \ out field tests on materials shall be provided\\n        without any extra cost.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"       1.    MATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP:     The whole of the materials used on the work\",\n  \"retained_lines\"\

- kind: parameter
  text: Storage facilities for materials like cement must be safe, dry, weather-proof with raised wooden flooring,
    and maintained with at least one month's supply during active construction, arranged in chronological order
    of receipt.

- kind: parameter
  text: Concrete works require weigh batching (or approved batch mixing plants), mixing times of at least 2 minutes
    for machine mixing, OPC cement complying with IS 8112 stored in stacks not exceeding 10 bags high, fine aggregates
    conforming to IS 383 with fineness modulus between 2.60 and 3.20, and coarse aggregates of 20 mm and down size
    for RCC work unless otherwise stated.
  evidence_ids:
  - J406cfa054c2884b8
  - J73e1bb795fa498c3
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    heading: Clause 6 — Cement Concrete Plain and Reinforced
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"6\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"6.\
      \   CEMENT CONCRETE PLAIN AND REINFORCED:\\n\\n            A) Cement:  Cement shall comply in every respect\
      \ with the requirements of the latest\\n                 publication of IS: 8112 and unless otherwise specified,\
      \ ordinary Portland cement shall\\n             be used. No other make of cement but that approved by the\
      \ Engineer will be allowed\\n             on works and the source of supply not be changed without approval\
      \ of the Engineer in\\n                   writing. Test certificates to show that the cement used fully complies\
      \ with the relevant\\n                IS specifications shall be submitted to the Engineer and not withstanding\
      \ this, the\\n               Engineer may at their discretion order that the cement brought to site and which\
      \ they\\n            may consider damaged or of doubtful quality for any reasons whatsoever shall be re-\\\
      n                tested in an approved testing laboratory and fresh certificate of its soundness shall be\\\
      n               produced. Cement ordered for retesting shall not be used for any work pending results\\n \
      \                 of retest.\\n             Cement shall be stored and neatly packed in piles not exceeding\
      \ 10 bags high in\\n               weather proof sheds with raised wooden plank flooring to prevent deterioration\
      \ by\\n             dampness or intrusion of foreign matter. It shall be stored in such a way as to allow\\\
      n                the removal and use of cement in chronological order of receipt, i.e. the first received\\\
      n               being first used. Cement deteriorated and/or clotted shall not be used on work but shall\\\
      n             be removed at once from the site.\\n\\n           B) Fine Aggregates: Sand shall conform to\
      \ IS: 383. It shall pass through 1.5 sieve 4.75\\n         mm (3/16 B.S) test sieve leaving a residue not\
      \ more than 5%. It shall be from a natural\\n               source or crushed stone screedings.  It shall\
      \ be washed  if directed to reduce the\\n               percentage of deleterious substances to acceptable\
      \ limits. Sand shall not contain any\\n                 trace of salt and sand containing any trace of salt\
      \ shall be rejected.\\n             The fine aggregate for concrete shall be graded within limits as specified\
      \ in IS: 383\\n             and the fineness modules shall range between 2.60 to 3.20. The fine aggregate\
      \ shall\\n               stacked carefully on a clean hard dry surface so that  it will not get mixed up with\\\
      n                 deleterious foreign materials. If such a surface is not available a platform of planks or\\\
      n                corrugated sheets or brick floor or concrete floor shall be prepared. Sand shall be\\n  \
      \           added in the desired proportion as required for the strength specified, with suitable\\n     \
      \            correction for bulking.\\n\\n           C) Coarse Aggregates:    Coarse aggregate shall conform\
      \ to IS: 383. It shall consist\\n                  of crushed or broken stone, 95% of which shall, be retained\
      \ on 4.75 mm is test sieve.\\n                          It shall be obtained from crushed granite, trap, basalt\
      \ or similar approved stones from\\n              approved quarry. Coarse aggregate shall be chemically inert\
      \ when mixed with cement\\n           & shall be angular in shape and free from soft friable thin porous laminated\
      \ or flaky\\n                pieces. It shall be free from dust and other foreign matter. Gravel/shingle of\
      \ desired\\n                grading may be permitted as a substitute in part or full in plain cement concrete\
      \ if the\\n               Engineer are otherwise satisfied about the quality of aggregate.\\n\\n         \
      \  D) Water:    Water used for mixing concrete shall be fresh, clean, free from oil, salts,\\n           \
      \      acids, alkali and shall be in accordance with the clause 4.3 of IS 456 - 1978. The\\n             \
      \    contractor shall produce test results for approval by the Engineer for the mixing of\\n             \
      \  water proposed to be used on the job, before casting any concrete.\\n\\n            E) Mixing Of Concrete:\
      \    MACHINE  MIXED:  Aggregates  shall  be  accurately\\n             measured out in boxes and mixed dry\
      \ along with required cement, water shall then be\\n             added in measured quantity and mixing shall\
      \ be continued until there is uniform\\n                  distribution of the materials and the mass is uniform\
      \ in color and consistency but in no\\n              case shall the mixing be done for less than 2 minutes.\
      \ Only hopper loading mixer shall\\n             be used.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"       6.\
      \   CEMENT CONCRETE PLAIN AND REINFORCED:\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n  \

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"5\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"5.\
      \    SPECIFICATIONS FOR CONCRETE WORKS\\n\\n      Concrete General\\n      Wherever grade of concrete like\
      \ M15, M20 etc. are mentioned, the concrete mix shall be\\n       designed by the contractor and approval\
      \ of Engineer for the same shall be obtained.\\n      Weigh batching shall be adopted for producing the concrete.\
      \ Obtaining of concrete from\\n       concrete batch mix plant will be permitted. Contractor shall obtain\
      \ approval of Engineer\\n         for each such batching plant. Proportion of ordinary cement concrete will\
      \ be expressed\\n      as l:3:6, 1:2:4, 1:1.5:3 etc. in the specification. The first figure will be the quantity\
      \ of\\n      cement by volume, the second figure will be dry coarse sand (fine aggregate) by volume\\n   \
      \   and the third figure will be the quantity of course aggregate by volume. Cement shall be\\n      measured\
      \ by weight. The weight is to be derived on the basis that 1 cubic meter of\\n      cement will weigh 1440\
      \ kgs., or one full bag of 50 kgs. will be assumed to be 35 ltrs.\\n      when the sand is wet or moist suitable\
      \ correction for bulking is to be given while\\n        proportioning. Engineer may allow measuring cement\
      \ by volume.\\n       Unless otherwise specified the rates for all RCC will be exclusive or reinforcements.\\\
      n       Reinforcements will be paid for separate1y.\\n       Unless, otherwise stated, for all RCC work the\
      \ size of course aggregate will be 20 mm\\n      and down size.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"5. \
      \   SPECIFICATIONS FOR CONCRETE WORKS\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\\n      sets\
      \ and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected\\n      from drying\
      \ wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\n       of any kind and generally\
      \ fit for drinking.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\

      : \"      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\"\n   \

    json_record_id: null

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"9\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"9.\
      \   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\\n       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\\n       of any of the ingredients.\
      \ If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\\n      remixed before being placed. Normally\
      \ not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\\n      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    }\n  ],\n\
      \  \"heading_line\": \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\
      ,\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"       of any of the ingredients. If segregation\
      \ does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 48,\n      \"text\": \"      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse\
      \ between\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"\

    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"7\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"7.\
      \  CONSISTENCY:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Only sufficient water giving\
      \ due allowance for the moisture content of aggregate shall be\\n      added to the cement and aggregates\
      \ during mixing to produce a mixture of sufficient\\n        workability to enable  it to be well consolidated,\
      \ to be worked into the corners of the\\n       shuttering and around the reinforcements where there is reinforcement\
      \ to give the\\n       specified finish and to have specified strength.\\n       Normally for every 50 kg\
      \ of cement total water including moisture content of aggregate\\n       should not be more than 34 liters\
      \ for 1:3:6 mix 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix. 30 liters for 1:1½:3\\n      and 27 liters for 1:1:2.\\n        \
      \    If  difficulty be experienced in placing the concrete of specified mix and approved\\n       consistency\
      \ between and below reinforcement bars, in the bottom of beams and similar\\n        situations, the concrete\
      \ shall have improved workability by increasing the proportion of\\n       water and corresponding additional\
      \ quantity of cement and using aggregates of smaller\\n        size than specified as directed by Engineer\
      \ for which no extra be paid.\\n      The consistency shall be determined by making trial mixtures with dried\
      \ aggregates, or\\n      when so instructed by test of laboratory made test cubes under the direction of Engineer.\\\
      n       Consistency may be measured by slump test using a standard cone or the Engineer may\\n        direct\
      \ the use of any other means of testing the consistency.\\n            If the apparatus used for the slump\
      \ test is a standard cone when filled. It shall be raised\\n         vertically clear of the concrete and\
      \ the measurement of the slump shall be 300 mm minus\\n       the height of the slumped cone of concrete.\
      \ Care shall be taken to prevent vibration of\\n       the samples being tested. The following slumps shall\
      \ be adopted for different kind of\\n       works:\\n\\n     WITH/WITHOUT VIBRATOR\\n        1.   Mass concrete\
      \ in RCC foundation, footings & retaining walls. 10 mm to 25 mm 80\\n       mm\\n        2.   Beams, slabs\
      \ and columns 25 mm to 40 mm 100 mm to 125 mm simply reinforced\\n        3.   Thin RCC section or 40 mm to\
      \ 50 mm 125 mm to 150 mm section with congested\\n              steel\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\":\
      \ \"          7.  CONSISTENCY:\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"source_line_number\"\

      \ of the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \" \

      \ not be more than 34 liters for 1:3:6 mix 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix. 30 liters for 1:1½:3\"\n    },\n    {\n\

      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \  If  difficulty be experienced in placing the concrete of specified mix and approved\"\n    },\n    {\n\

      \ and below reinforcement bars, in the bottom of beams and similar\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\

      \ 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"            If the apparatus used for the slump\
      \ test is a standard cone when filled. It shall be raised\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n    \
      \  \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"         vertically clear of the concrete and the measurement\
      \ of the slump shall be 300 mm minus\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 25,\n      \"text\": \"       the height of the slumped cone of concrete. Care shall be taken to prevent\
      \ vibration of\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"       the samples being tested. The following slumps shall be adopted for different kind of\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"       works:\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"     WITH/WITHOUT VIBRATOR\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"text\": \"        1.\
      \   Mass concrete in RCC foundation, footings & retaining walls. 10 mm to 25 mm 80\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"       mm\"\n    },\n    {\n  \
      \    \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"        2.   Beams, slabs and\
      \ columns 25 mm to 40 mm 100 mm to 125 mm simply reinforced\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"        3.   Thin RCC section or 40 mm to 50 mm 125 mm\
      \ to 150 mm section with congested\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 34,\n      \"text\": \"              steel\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\\n        yield\
      \ strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966, Fe-500 or\\n      as called for on\
      \ the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\\n       mild steel wire mesh\
      \ conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\\n        materials, mill scale, loose\
      \ rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\\n       schedules for approval of the Engineer\
      \ prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall\\n        indicate the accurate dimensions and bending\
      \ of bars as called for on the structural\\n       drawings. Fabrication shall be accurately done to the dimensions,\
      \ spacing and ensuring\\n      minimum cover as called for on structural drawings. All steel shall be rigidly\
      \ held in place\\n       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape,\
      \ M.S.\\n       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order to ensure accurate positioning  of\\n     \
      \  reinforcement.\\n         All reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and\
      \ firmly wired at\\n        least one day, prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor\
      \ shall also\\n       ensure that all conduits and inserts are in position before placing concrete.\\n   \
      \   Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with a radius of not\\n        less\
      \ than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at least four\\n       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\\n       diameters plus an extension\
      \ of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\\n      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times\
      \ the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\n      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints\
      \ at the same location. Only where\\n        specifically shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have\
      \ welded joints. Rate quoted\\n         for steel reinforcement work shall be deemed to include the cost of\
      \ such welding\\n       wherever specified.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel\
      \ reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n   \

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"13\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"\
      13.   STRENGTH OF ORDINARY CONCRETE:       The contractors have to see that\\n             proper materials\
      \ are used and the proportion and the correct water cement ratio just\\n                sufficient for the\
      \ workability are maintained to see that the following minimum strength of\\n             concrete are obtained.\\\
      n           To verify this, test cubes from the concrete used should be made and tested. The\\n          \
      \   frequency of testing and the acceptability criteria will be according to IS 456 and IS 516.\"\n    }\n\
      \  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"      13.   STRENGTH OF ORDINARY CONCRETE:       The contractors have to see\
      \ that\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n  \

      pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"                sufficient for the workability\
      \ are maintained to see that the following minimum strength of\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\

      \      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"           To verify this,\

  - id: J920a9f596916d4bf
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5
    heading: Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 37
    - 38
    - 39
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /technical_specifications/13
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"14\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"\
      14   CONCRETE FORMWORK AND CENTERING:\\n\\n      14.1   General:\\n           Form work shall be rigidly constructed\
      \ of 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets\\n                for column, pedestals, beams, walls and\
      \ fascia and steel or plywood for roof slab,\\n             canopies, floors, cover slabs, retaining walls,\
      \ etc. Shuttering faces in contact with the\\n             concrete shall be free from adhering grout, projecting\
      \ nails, splits or other defects that\\n             are likely to mar the concrete surface. The shuttering\
      \ shall be erected on battens, beams\\n           and steel props properly cross-braced so as to make the\
      \ form work rigid.\\n           Form work shall be erected true to line and levels and to the required shapes\
      \ as called\\n                for and shall be able to carry without deformation the full weight of wet concrete\
      \ and live\\n              loads. It shall also withstand the effect of vibration without deflection, bulging,\
      \ distortion\\n              or loosening of its component parts. Details of centering and form work shall\
      \ be subject\\n               to the approval of the Engineer. The completed form work shall also be subject\
      \ to the\\n             approval by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor shall be\\\
      n              responsible for sufficiency and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds.\\n        \
      \         All joints shall be sufficiently tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be\\n\
      \             provided at all corners wherever called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer.\\\
      n            Openings may .be given with the approval of Engineer at convenient places for washing\\n    \
      \       down all the rubbish. These are to be closed before concreting.\\n\\n      14.2  Surface Treatment\
      \ of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact with\\n             concrete shall be plastic\
      \ coated of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly\\n            cleaned and coated before each\
      \ use.\\n      14.3  Removal of Form Work:     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\\\
      n           and shall be eased off carefully in order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\\\
      n           Forms shall not be disturbed until, concrete had adequately hardened to take up its own\\n   \
      \          weight and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall forms be struck\\n     \
      \            until the concrete reaches strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete may\\\
      n             subjected at the time of striking. The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\\n \
      \          them the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\\n      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\\\
      n            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\\n     \
      \          to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall be repaired by\\n      \
      \      removal and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately, measurement will be taken\\\
      n       according to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter.\\n         All aggregate\
      \ are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates will be\\n       tested as frequently\
      \ as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the same\\n      as adopted in the mix design.\
      \ They must be stored on clean platform made for the\\n       purpose.\\n       Concrete shall be weighed\
      \ batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\\n      checked with standard weights periodically.\
      \ The conversions of weights to volume will be\\n       allowed by the Engineer under special circumstances.\
      \ Despite the design for several\\n       mixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used\
      \ for the different\\n       grades of the concrete:-\\n      Cement concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40\
      \ Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:3:6)                 - 4.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags\
      \ per cum.\\n     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per cum.\\n     RCC              (1:1:2)\
      \            -10.80 Bags per cum\\n\\n14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\\n       Immediately on removal of form\
      \ work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined by\\n       the contractor and any bone combs or other\
      \ imperfections shall be brought to the notice\\n        of the Engineer. The acceptability or otherwise of\
      \ such defective concrete shall be at the\\n       sole discretion of the Engineer who may direct the contractor\
      \ to repair the defective work\\n        or ask for demolition and replacement of such defective work at the\
      \ risk and cost of the\\n        contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 39,\n      \"text\": \"\
      CONCRETE WORK\\n                          LIST OF BUREAU OF INDIAN STANDARDS\\n\\n\\n              Sl\\n \
      \                    IS No.                               Subject\\n         No\\n            1   383-1970\
      \    Coarse and fine aggregate from Natural source for concrete (2nd\\n                                  \
      \  revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            2   456-2000    Code of practice  for plain and reinforced\
      \ concrete  (3rd revision)\\n                          (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            3\
      \   516-1959    Method of sampling and analysis of concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            4   1200  \
      \ (Part  Method of measurement of building and civil engineering work Part 2\\n                          \
      \  II)            (concrete works). (3rd revision) (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n               1974\\\
      n            5   1322-1993   Bitumen  felt  for water proofing and damp proofing  (4th revision)\\n      \
      \                            (previously 13220-1982)\\n\\n            6   1786-1966   High  strength  deformed\
      \  steel  bars  and  wires  for  concrete\\n                                 reinforcement.\\n           \
      \ 7   1791-1985   Batch type concrete mixers. (2nd revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            8   2386-1963\
      \   Method of test for aggregate for concrete work.\\n\\n                                     a)  Part 1 particle\
      \ size and shape (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed\\n                                 1990\\n                    \
      \                 b)  Part 2  Estimation  of  deleterious  materials and  organic\\n                     \
      \                      impurities (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n                                     \
      \   c)  Part 3 Specific gravity, density, voids, absorption and bulking\\n                               \
      \  – Reaffirmed 1990.l\\n                                     d)  Part 4 Mechanical properties (Amendments\
      \ 3) Reaffirmed\\n                                     1990.\\n\\n            9   2505-1980   General  requirements\
      \  for  concrete  vibrators  immersion  type.\\n                               Reaffirmed 1993.\\n\\n    \
      \       10  2505-1985   General requirements  for screed board  concrete  vibrators.  (1st\\n            \
      \                        revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n           11  2645-1975    Integral  cement  water\
      \   proofing  components   (1st   revision)\\n                          (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\\
      n\\n           12  2686-1977    Cinder as fine aggregate  for use  in lime concrete  (1st revision)\\n   \
      \                       (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\n\\n           13  3812-1981    Fly ash for use as\
      \ Pozzolana and admixtures (1st revision) Reaffirmed\\n                              1992.\\n\\n         \
      \  14  4656-1968   Form vibrators for concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n           15  8112        43 grade\
      \ OPC\\n\\n           16  9103-1979    Admixture for concrete Reaffirmed 1990.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\

      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"      14.2  Surface Treatment\
      \ of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact with\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"             concrete shall be plastic coated\
      \ of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 48,\n      \"text\": \"            cleaned and coated before each use.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"      14.3  Removal of\
      \ Form Work:     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 50,\n      \"text\": \"           and shall be eased off carefully in\
      \ order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"           Forms shall not be disturbed until, concrete\
      \ had adequately hardened to take up its own\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"             weight and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall\
      \ forms be struck\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                 until the concrete reaches strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete\
      \ may\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \          subjected at the time of striking. The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"           them\
      \ the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 56,\n      \"text\": \"      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum\
      \ 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 58,\n      \"text\": \"               to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall\
      \ be repaired by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 59,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"            removal and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 62,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 63,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n   \
      \   \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"            If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately, measurement will be taken\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"       according\
      \ to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"         All aggregate are to conform strictly to IS\
      \ specification IS 383. The aggregates will be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 9,\n      \"text\": \"       tested as frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification\
      \ is the same\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      as adopted in the mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"       purpose.\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"       Concrete\
      \ shall be weighed batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"      checked with standard weights\
      \ periodically. The conversions of weights to volume will be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"       allowed by the Engineer under special circumstances.\
      \ Despite the design for several\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\":\
      \ 15,\n      \"text\": \"       mixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used for the\
      \ different\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"       grades of the concrete:-\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 17,\n      \"text\": \"      Cement concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40 Bags per cum.\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:3:6)                 - 4.40 Bags per cum.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 19,\n      \"text\": \"      Cement concrete (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags per cum.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 21,\n      \"text\": \"     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per cum.\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"     RCC              (1:1:2)\
      \            -10.80 Bags per cum\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\":\
      \ 23,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 25,\n      \"text\": \"       Immediately on removal of form work, the surface of the concrete shall be\
      \ examined by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"       the contractor and any bone combs or other imperfections shall be brought to the notice\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"        of the\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"8\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"8.\
      \    PLACING AND COMPACTING:         Method  of  placing  shall be such as  to\\n       preclude segregation\
      \ and as far as practicable the placing shall be continuous.\\n       Special care shall be taken in accordance\
      \ with IS 456 while laying concrete under\\n       extreme weather. Concrete, during the operation of placing\
      \ shall be thoroughly worked\\n       around the reinforcements, embedded fixtures, spaded against corners\
      \ of the formwork\\n      by punning, rodding, or by any other approved means and thoroughly compacted by\\\
      n       mechanical vibrators. The number and type of vibrator to be used shall be subject to the\\n      \
      \ approval of the Engineer, and in general immersion type vibrators shall be used.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"8.    PLACING AND COMPACTING:         Method  of  placing  shall be such as  to\",\n  \"retained_lines\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"8.    PLACING AND\
      \ COMPACTING:         Method  of  placing  shall be such as  to\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"       preclude segregation and as far as practicable\
      \ the placing shall be continuous.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"       Special care shall be taken in accordance with IS 456 while laying concrete\
      \ under\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n      \"text\": \" \
      \      extreme weather. Concrete, during the operation of placing shall be thoroughly worked\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"       around the reinforcements,\
      \ embedded fixtures, spaded against corners of the formwork\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 41,\n      \"text\": \"      by punning, rodding, or by any other approved means\
      \ and thoroughly compacted by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 42,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"       mechanical vibrators. The number and type of vibrator to be used shall be subject\
      \ to the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 43,\n      \"text\": \"\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"11\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      11.  CONSTRUCTION JOINTS:   Construction joints in exposed concrete work shall be made\\n      only where\
      \ shown on the drawings or directed by the Engineer and shall be in accordance\\n      with the details shown\
      \ or approved by the Engineer. The procedure given in clause\\n      20.1.4.2 of IS; 456-1978 shall be followed\
      \ for general guidance. All laitance shall be\\n     removed from the concrete before  it is allowed to fully\
      \ harden. The removal shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"effected by scrubbing\
      \ the concrete surface with wire and with bristle brushes and washing\\n          down to expose clearly the\
      \ aggregate. However, care shall be taken to avoid dislodgement\\n              of particles of aggregate.\\\
      n                       If concrete has been allowed to harden excessively the surface shall be chipped over\
      \ its\\n            whole surface to a depth of at least 10 mm and thereafter thoroughly washed. Before\\\
      n              fresh concrete is added on the construction joints, the surface of the old concrete shall\\\
      n           be thoroughly wetted and covered with a thin layer of cement mortar 1:2.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"\
      heading_line\": \"11.  CONSTRUCTION JOINTS:   Construction joints in exposed concrete work shall be made\"\
      ,\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 56,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"11.  CONSTRUCTION JOINTS:   Construction joints in exposed concrete work shall be made\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"      only where shown\
      \ on the drawings or directed by the Engineer and shall be in accordance\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\": \"      with the details shown or approved by the\
      \ Engineer. The procedure given in clause\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 59,\n      \"text\": \"      20.1.4.2 of IS; 456-1978 shall be followed for general guidance. All laitance\
      \ shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \     removed from the concrete before  it is allowed to fully harden. The removal shall be\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n  \
      \    \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 62,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 6,\n      \"text\": \"             effected by scrubbing the concrete surface with wire and with bristle\
      \ brushes and washing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"\
      text\": \"          down to expose clearly the aggregate. However, care shall be taken to avoid dislodgement\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"           \
      \   of particles of aggregate.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"                       If concrete has been allowed to harden excessively the surface shall\
      \ be chipped over its\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"            whole surface to a depth of at least 10 mm and thereafter thoroughly washed. Before\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \    fresh concrete is added on the construction joints, the surface of the old concrete shall\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"           be thoroughly\
      \ wetted and covered with a thin layer of cement mortar 1:2.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"title\": \"CONSTRUCTION JOINTS\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"9\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"9.\
      \   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\\n       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\\n       of any of the ingredients.\
      \ If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\\n      remixed before being placed. Normally\
      \ not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\\n      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    }\n  ],\n\
      \  \"heading_line\": \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\
      ,\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"       of any of the ingredients. If segregation\
      \ does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 48,\n      \"text\": \"      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse\
      \ between\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 50,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      TRANSPORTING\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Cement concrete after laying must be protected from damage until set and cured thereafter for not less than
    10 days using water free from sediments and fit for drinking.
  evidence_ids:
  - J2707c9c8171b144c
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\\n      sets\
      \ and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected\\n      from drying\
      \ wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\n       of any kind and generally\
      \ fit for drinking.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall\
      \ be protected\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"       of any kind\
      \ and generally fit for drinking.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 55,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      CURING\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Formwork must be rigidly constructed using 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets for columns, pedestals,
    beams, walls, and fascia, and steel or plywood for roof slabs, canopies, floors, cover slabs, and retaining
    walls.
  evidence_ids:
  - J920a9f596916d4bf
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C8badf2c45890e43c
  citations:
  - id: J920a9f596916d4bf
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5
    heading: Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 37
    - 38
    - 39
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /technical_specifications/13
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"14\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"\
      14   CONCRETE FORMWORK AND CENTERING:\\n\\n      14.1   General:\\n           Form work shall be rigidly constructed\
      \ of 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets\\n                for column, pedestals, beams, walls and\
      \ fascia and steel or plywood for roof slab,\\n             canopies, floors, cover slabs, retaining walls,\
      \ etc. Shuttering faces in contact with the\\n             concrete shall be free from adhering grout, projecting\
      \ nails, splits or other defects that\\n             are likely to mar the concrete surface. The shuttering\
      \ shall be erected on battens, beams\\n           and steel props properly cross-braced so as to make the\
      \ form work rigid.\\n           Form work shall be erected true to line and levels and to the required shapes\
      \ as called\\n                for and shall be able to carry without deformation the full weight of wet concrete\
      \ and live\\n              loads. It shall also withstand the effect of vibration without deflection, bulging,\
      \ distortion\\n              or loosening of its component parts. Details of centering and form work shall\
      \ be subject\\n               to the approval of the Engineer. The completed form work shall also be subject\
      \ to the\\n             approval by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor shall be\\\
      n              responsible for sufficiency and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds.\\n        \
      \         All joints shall be sufficiently tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be\\n\
      \             provided at all corners wherever called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer.\\\
      n            Openings may .be given with the approval of Engineer at convenient places for washing\\n    \
      \       down all the rubbish. These are to be closed before concreting.\\n\\n      14.2  Surface Treatment\
      \ of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact with\\n             concrete shall be plastic\
      \ coated of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly\\n            cleaned and coated before each\
      \ use.\\n      14.3  Removal of Form Work:     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\\\
      n           and shall be eased off carefully in order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\\\
      n           Forms shall not be disturbed until, concrete had adequately hardened to take up its own\\n   \
      \          weight and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall forms be struck\\n     \
      \            until the concrete reaches strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete may\\\
      n             subjected at the time of striking. The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\\n \
      \          them the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\\n      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\\\
      n            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\\n     \
      \          to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall be repaired by\\n      \
      \      removal and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately, measurement will be taken\\\
      n       according to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter.\\n         All aggregate\
      \ are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates will be\\n       tested as frequently\
      \ as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the same\\n      as adopted in the mix design.\
      \ They must be stored on clean platform made for the\\n       purpose.\\n       Concrete shall be weighed\
      \ batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\\n      checked with standard weights periodically.\
      \ The conversions of weights to volume will be\\n       allowed by the Engineer under special circumstances.\
      \ Despite the design for several\\n       mixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used\
      \ for the different\\n       grades of the concrete:-\\n      Cement concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40\
      \ Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:3:6)                 - 4.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags\
      \ per cum.\\n     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per cum.\\n     RCC              (1:1:2)\
      \            -10.80 Bags per cum\\n\\n14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\\n       Immediately on removal of form\
      \ work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined by\\n       the contractor and any bone combs or other\
      \ imperfections shall be brought to the notice\\n        of the Engineer. The acceptability or otherwise of\
      \ such defective concrete shall be at the\\n       sole discretion of the Engineer who may direct the contractor\
      \ to repair the defective work\\n        or ask for demolition and replacement of such defective work at the\
      \ risk and cost of the\\n        contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 39,\n      \"text\": \"\
      CONCRETE WORK\\n                          LIST OF BUREAU OF INDIAN STANDARDS\\n\\n\\n              Sl\\n \
      \                    IS No.                               Subject\\n         No\\n            1   383-1970\
      \    Coarse and fine aggregate from Natural source for concrete (2nd\\n                                  \
      \  revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            2   456-2000    Code of practice  for plain and reinforced\
      \ concrete  (3rd revision)\\n                          (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            3\
      \   516-1959    Method of sampling and analysis of concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            4   1200  \
      \ (Part  Method of measurement of building and civil engineering work Part 2\\n                          \
      \  II)            (concrete works). (3rd revision) (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n               1974\\\
      n            5   1322-1993   Bitumen  felt  for water proofing and damp proofing  (4th revision)\\n      \
      \                            (previously 13220-1982)\\n\\n            6   1786-1966   High  strength  deformed\
      \  steel  bars  and  wires  for  concrete\\n                                 reinforcement.\\n           \
      \ 7   1791-1985   Batch type concrete mixers. (2nd revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            8   2386-1963\
      \   Method of test for aggregate for concrete work.\\n\\n                                     a)  Part 1 particle\
      \ size and shape (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed\\n                                 1990\\n                    \
      \                 b)  Part 2  Estimation  of  deleterious  materials and  organic\\n                     \
      \                      impurities (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n                                     \
      \   c)  Part 3 Specific gravity, density, voids, absorption and bulking\\n                               \
      \  – Reaffirmed 1990.l\\n                                     d)  Part 4 Mechanical properties (Amendments\
      \ 3) Reaffirmed\\n                                     1990.\\n\\n            9   2505-1980   General  requirements\
      \  for  concrete  vibrators  immersion  type.\\n                               Reaffirmed 1993.\\n\\n    \
      \       10  2505-1985   General requirements  for screed board  concrete  vibrators.  (1st\\n            \
      \                        revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n           11  2645-1975    Integral  cement  water\
      \   proofing  components   (1st   revision)\\n                          (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\\
      n\\n           12  2686-1977    Cinder as fine aggregate  for use  in lime concrete  (1st revision)\\n   \
      \                       (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\n\\n           13  3812-1981    Fly ash for use as\
      \ Pozzolana and admixtures (1st revision) Reaffirmed\\n                              1992.\\n\\n         \
      \  14  4656-1968   Form vibrators for concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n           15  8112        43 grade\
      \ OPC\\n\\n           16  9103-1979    Admixture for concrete Reaffirmed 1990.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\

- kind: parameter
  text: 'Minimum cement quantities for concrete grades are specified as: 3.40 bags/cum for 1:4:8, 4.40 bags/cum
    for 1:3:6, 6.40 bags/cum for 1:2:4, 8.00 bags/cum for 1:1.5:3 plain and RCC, and 10.80 bags/cum for RCC 1:1:2.'
  evidence_ids:

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"14\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"\
      14   CONCRETE FORMWORK AND CENTERING:\\n\\n      14.1   General:\\n           Form work shall be rigidly constructed\
      \ of 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets\\n                for column, pedestals, beams, walls and\
      \ fascia and steel or plywood for roof slab,\\n             canopies, floors, cover slabs, retaining walls,\
      \ etc. Shuttering faces in contact with the\\n             concrete shall be free from adhering grout, projecting\
      \ nails, splits or other defects that\\n             are likely to mar the concrete surface. The shuttering\
      \ shall be erected on battens, beams\\n           and steel props properly cross-braced so as to make the\
      \ form work rigid.\\n           Form work shall be erected true to line and levels and to the required shapes\
      \ as called\\n                for and shall be able to carry without deformation the full weight of wet concrete\
      \ and live\\n              loads. It shall also withstand the effect of vibration without deflection, bulging,\
      \ distortion\\n              or loosening of its component parts. Details of centering and form work shall\
      \ be subject\\n               to the approval of the Engineer. The completed form work shall also be subject\
      \ to the\\n             approval by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor shall be\\\
      n              responsible for sufficiency and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds.\\n        \
      \         All joints shall be sufficiently tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be\\n\
      \             provided at all corners wherever called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer.\\\
      n            Openings may .be given with the approval of Engineer at convenient places for washing\\n    \
      \       down all the rubbish. These are to be closed before concreting.\\n\\n      14.2  Surface Treatment\
      \ of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact with\\n             concrete shall be plastic\
      \ coated of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly\\n            cleaned and coated before each\
      \ use.\\n      14.3  Removal of Form Work:     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\\\
      n           and shall be eased off carefully in order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\\\
      n           Forms shall not be disturbed until, concrete had adequately hardened to take up its own\\n   \
      \          weight and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall forms be struck\\n     \
      \            until the concrete reaches strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete may\\\
      n             subjected at the time of striking. The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\\n \
      \          them the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\\n      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\\\
      n            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\\n     \
      \          to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall be repaired by\\n      \
      \      removal and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately, measurement will be taken\\\
      n       according to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter.\\n         All aggregate\
      \ are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates will be\\n       tested as frequently\
      \ as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the same\\n      as adopted in the mix design.\
      \ They must be stored on clean platform made for the\\n       purpose.\\n       Concrete shall be weighed\
      \ batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\\n      checked with standard weights periodically.\
      \ The conversions of weights to volume will be\\n       allowed by the Engineer under special circumstances.\
      \ Despite the design for several\\n       mixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used\
      \ for the different\\n       grades of the concrete:-\\n      Cement concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40\
      \ Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:3:6)                 - 4.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags\
      \ per cum.\\n     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per cum.\\n     RCC              (1:1:2)\
      \            -10.80 Bags per cum\\n\\n14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\\n       Immediately on removal of form\
      \ work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined by\\n       the contractor and any bone combs or other\
      \ imperfections shall be brought to the notice\\n        of the Engineer. The acceptability or otherwise of\
      \ such defective concrete shall be at the\\n       sole discretion of the Engineer who may direct the contractor\
      \ to repair the defective work\\n        or ask for demolition and replacement of such defective work at the\
      \ risk and cost of the\\n        contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 39,\n      \"text\": \"\
      CONCRETE WORK\\n                          LIST OF BUREAU OF INDIAN STANDARDS\\n\\n\\n              Sl\\n \
      \                    IS No.                               Subject\\n         No\\n            1   383-1970\
      \    Coarse and fine aggregate from Natural source for concrete (2nd\\n                                  \
      \  revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            2   456-2000    Code of practice  for plain and reinforced\
      \ concrete  (3rd revision)\\n                          (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            3\
      \   516-1959    Method of sampling and analysis of concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n            4   1200  \
      \ (Part  Method of measurement of building and civil engineering work Part 2\\n                          \
      \  II)            (concrete works). (3rd revision) (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed 1991.\\n               1974\\\
      n            5   1322-1993   Bitumen  felt  for water proofing and damp proofing  (4th revision)\\n      \
      \                            (previously 13220-1982)\\n\\n            6   1786-1966   High  strength  deformed\
      \  steel  bars  and  wires  for  concrete\\n                                 reinforcement.\\n           \
      \ 7   1791-1985   Batch type concrete mixers. (2nd revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n            8   2386-1963\
      \   Method of test for aggregate for concrete work.\\n\\n                                     a)  Part 1 particle\
      \ size and shape (Amendments 2) Reaffirmed\\n                                 1990\\n                    \
      \                 b)  Part 2  Estimation  of  deleterious  materials and  organic\\n                     \
      \                      impurities (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n                                     \
      \   c)  Part 3 Specific gravity, density, voids, absorption and bulking\\n                               \
      \  – Reaffirmed 1990.l\\n                                     d)  Part 4 Mechanical properties (Amendments\
      \ 3) Reaffirmed\\n                                     1990.\\n\\n            9   2505-1980   General  requirements\
      \  for  concrete  vibrators  immersion  type.\\n                               Reaffirmed 1993.\\n\\n    \
      \       10  2505-1985   General requirements  for screed board  concrete  vibrators.  (1st\\n            \
      \                        revision) Reaffirmed 1990.\\n\\n           11  2645-1975    Integral  cement  water\
      \   proofing  components   (1st   revision)\\n                          (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\\
      n\\n           12  2686-1977    Cinder as fine aggregate  for use  in lime concrete  (1st revision)\\n   \
      \                       (Amendments 1) Reaffirmed 1992.\\n\\n           13  3812-1981    Fly ash for use as\
      \ Pozzolana and admixtures (1st revision) Reaffirmed\\n                              1992.\\n\\n         \
      \  14  4656-1968   Form vibrators for concrete. Reaffirmed 1991.\\n\\n           15  8112        43 grade\
      \ OPC\\n\\n           16  9103-1979    Admixture for concrete Reaffirmed 1990.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\

      \ be subject to the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n      \"\
      text\": \"             approval by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor shall be\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"           \
      \   responsible for sufficiency and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds.\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 41,\n      \"text\": \"                 All joints shall\
      \ be sufficiently tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 42,\n      \"text\": \"             provided at all corners wherever\
      \ called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 43,\n      \"text\": \"            Openings may .be given with the approval\
      \ of Engineer at convenient places for washing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 44,\n      \"text\": \"           down all the rubbish. These are to be closed before concreting.\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"      14.2  Surface Treatment\
      \ of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact with\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"             concrete shall be plastic coated\
      \ of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 48,\n      \"text\": \"            cleaned and coated before each use.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"      14.3  Removal of\
      \ Form Work:     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 50,\n      \"text\": \"           and shall be eased off carefully in\
      \ order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"           Forms shall not be disturbed until, concrete\
      \ had adequately hardened to take up its own\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"             weight and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall\
      \ forms be struck\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                 until the concrete reaches strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete\
      \ may\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \          subjected at the time of striking. The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"           them\
      \ the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 56,\n      \"text\": \"      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum\
      \ 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 58,\n      \"text\": \"               to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall\
      \ be repaired by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 59,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"            removal and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n \

      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n   \
      \   \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"            If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately, measurement will be taken\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"       according\

      \      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"         All aggregate are to conform strictly to IS\

      : 9,\n      \"text\": \"       tested as frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification\

      : \"      as adopted in the mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the\"\n    },\n   \

      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 38,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"       Concrete\

- kind: parameter
  text: Steel reinforcement shall consist of mild steel of tested quality or high yield strength deformed bars of
    grade Fe-415 conforming to IS:1786-1966, Fe-500, or as specified on drawings, with laps generally equal to 50
    times the bar diameter and not more than 33% of bars lapped at the same location.
