    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\\n        yield\
      \ strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966, Fe-500 or\\n      as called for on\
      \ the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\\n       mild steel wire mesh\
      \ conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\\n        materials, mill scale, loose\
      \ rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\\n       schedules for approval of the Engineer\
      \ prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall\\n        indicate the accurate dimensions and bending\
      \ of bars as called for on the structural\\n       drawings. Fabrication shall be accurately done to the dimensions,\
      \ spacing and ensuring\\n      minimum cover as called for on structural drawings. All steel shall be rigidly\
      \ held in place\\n       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape,\
      \ M.S.\\n       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order to ensure accurate positioning  of\\n     \
      \  reinforcement.\\n         All reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and\
      \ firmly wired at\\n        least one day, prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor\
      \ shall also\\n       ensure that all conduits and inserts are in position before placing concrete.\\n   \
      \   Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with a radius of not\\n        less\
      \ than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at least four\\n       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\\n       diameters plus an extension\
      \ of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\\n      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times\
      \ the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\n      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints\
      \ at the same location. Only where\\n        specifically shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have\
      \ welded joints. Rate quoted\\n         for steel reinforcement work shall be deemed to include the cost of\
      \ such welding\\n       wherever specified.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel\
      \ reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement\
      \ shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"        yield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966,\
      \ Fe-500 or\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \      as called for on the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"       mild steel wire\
      \ mesh conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"        materials, mill scale, loose rust, oil\
      \ or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"       schedules for approval of the Engineer prior to commencement of fabrication.\
      \ These shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"        indicate the accurate dimensions and bending of bars as called for on the structural\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"       drawings. Fabrication\
      \ shall be accurately done to the dimensions, spacing and ensuring\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"      minimum cover as called for on structural drawings.\
      \ All steel shall be rigidly held in place\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 15,\n      \"text\": \"       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required\
      \ shape, M.S.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order to ensure accurate positioning  of\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"       reinforcement.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"         All\
      \ reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and firmly wired at\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"        least one day,\
      \ prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor shall also\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"       ensure that all conduits and inserts are\
      \ in position before placing concrete.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 21,\n      \"text\": \"      Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with a\
      \ radius of not\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"        less than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at least four\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"       diameters plus an extension of 12 bar diameters.\
      \ Unless otherwise shown or specified\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 25,\n      \"text\": \"      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times the diameter of bar. Not more than\
      \ 33%. (Thirty-\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints at the same location. Only where\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"        specifically\
      \ shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have welded joints. Rate quoted\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"         for steel reinforcement work shall be\
      \ deemed to include the cost of such welding\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 29,\n      \"text\": \"       wherever specified.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 30,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      REINFORCEMENT\"\n}"

- kind: summary
  text: Volume 2 Part 4B establishes technical specifications and execution standards for rehabilitation and restoration
    works (such as front walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns) required for safety and functional reasons following
    Building Condition Studies (BCS) under BMRCL Phase 3 Tender Package P1, with measurements restricted to buildings
    completed under the BCS consultant's supervision.
  evidence_ids:
  - J28896027b0708774
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C3a25f11eab171fdf

    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 33,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"180\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n    PART – B: SPECIFICATIONS FOR REHABILITATION AND\
      \ RESTORATION WORKS\\n\\n      Restoration works for safety reasons.\\n       1.    The BMRCL may appoint\
      \ consultant/Engineer to conduct Building Condition Study (BCS)\\n          and suggest sequence and methodology\
      \ of demolition including remedial measures\\n             required to be undertaken prior to demolition and/or\
      \ during demolition and/or post\\n             demolition.\\n                    It is anticipated that, certain\
      \ structures will be required to be constructed for safety and\\n              functional reasons as a result\
      \ of Building Condition Study. The structures anticipated are\\n               front walls, doors, windows,\
      \ beams and columns etc.\\n          The BMRCL has identified few items which are to be executed in this contract.\
      \ These\\n            items  shall be as per KPWD Common Schedule  of Rates  for the year 2023-24.\\n    \
      \       Measurements will be recorded for only those buildings completed under the supervision\\n        \
      \      of consultant/Engineer appointed for Building Condition Study.\\n       2.      All Standards, Technical\
      \ Specifications and Codes of Practice referred to shall be latest\\n             editions including all applicable\
      \ official amendments and revisions. The contractor shall\\n         make available at site all relevant KPWD\
      \ Specifications and Indian Standard Codes of\\n             practice as applicable.\\n              In case\
      \ of discrepancy among Standard codes of practice, Technical Specifications and\\n             provisions\
      \ in Sub clauses in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n              Specifications’,\
      \ the order of precedence will be as below:\\n\\n                               i)  Provision in this ‘Employer\
      \ Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n                    Specifications’\\n                        \
      \         ii)  Technical Specifications\\n                                  iii) KPWD Specifications\\n  \
      \                  v) MORTH Specifications\\n                           vii) Standard Codes of Practice\\\
      n\\n       3.    The utilities which are not to be diverted but require supporting, proper supporting be done\\\
      n           so that they are not damaged along their branches. All Precautions shall be taken while\\n   \
      \         handling the utilities. The Contractor shall be liable for any damages in this behalf.\\n      \
      \ 4.    Before carrying out the work at site, necessary permissions as required from various local\\n    \
      \        agencies / authorities shall be required to be obtained by the contractor. The Employer\\n      \
      \        shall assist only by way of issue of necessary support letters.\\n       5.    The work has to be\
      \ executed carefully without damaging the existing surface/over\\n            ground/underground utilities.\
      \ The contractor shall execute electrical and plumbing works\\n            through Licensed Electrical/Plumbing\
      \ contractors.\\n       6.    The Contractor shall strictly adhere to the working drawings or detail drawings\
      \ and written\\n              instructions of the Engineer.\\n\\n     TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS\\n\\n     \
      \  1.    MATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP:     The whole of the materials used on the work\\n               shall\
      \ be new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants\\n              \
      \ shall be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best,\\n       \
      \    most workman like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the\\n             Engineer.\\\
      n           The workmanship is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall\\n  \
      \           take all steps immediately to make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must\\n \
      \          be made of a special tradesman in all aspects of the work and allowances must be made\\n      \
      \          in the rates for the same.\\n\\n              Contractor shall maintain uniform quality and consistency\
      \ in workmanship throughout the\\n             execution of the work.\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail\
      \ Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 30 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 30 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\

      \        71.99996948242188,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        501.6673583984375,\n        84.39871978759766\n\

      \ WORKS \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00061798095703,\n        86.64318084716797,\n\

      \ a result of Building Condition Study.  The structures anticipated are \\nfront walls, doors, windows, beams\
      \ and columns etc. \\nThe BMRCL has identified few items which are to be executed in this contract.  These\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00117492675781,\n        213.242919921875,\n       \
      \ 539.230712890625,\n        350.19818115234375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"\
      items shall be as per KPWD Common Schedule of Rates for the year 2023-24. \\nMeasurements will be recorded\
      \ for only those buildings completed under the supervision \\nof consultant/Engineer appointed for Building\
      \ Condition Study.  \\n2.  \\nAll Standards, Technical Specifications and Codes of Practice referred to shall\
      \ be latest \\neditions including all applicable official amendments and revisions. The contractor shall \\\
      nmake available at site all relevant KPWD Specifications and Indian Standard Codes of \\npractice as applicable.\
      \ \\n \\nIn case of discrepancy among Standard codes of practice, Technical Specifications and \\nprovisions\
      \ in Sub clauses in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical \\nSpecifications’, the order of\
      \ precedence will be as below: \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00119018554688,\n \
      \       352.44268798828125,\n        539.2310180664062,\n        767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \" \\ni)    Provision in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical \\nSpecifications’\
      \ \\n \\nii)   Technical Specifications \\n \\niii)  KPWD Specifications \\n \\nv)   MORTH Specifications\
      \ \\n \\nvii) Standard Codes of Practice \\n \\n3.  \\nThe utilities which are not to be diverted but require\
      \ supporting, proper supporting be done \\nso that they are not damaged along their branches. All Precautions\
      \ shall be taken while \\nhandling the utilities.   The Contractor shall be liable for any damages in this\
      \ behalf. \\n4. \\nBefore carrying out the work at site, necessary permissions as required from various local\
      \ \\nagencies / authorities shall be required to be obtained by the contractor.  The Employer \\nshall assist\
      \ only by way of issue of necessary support letters.  \\n5. \\nThe work has to be executed carefully without\
      \ damaging the existing surface/over \\nground/underground utilities. The contractor shall execute electrical\
      \ and plumbing works \\nthrough Licensed Electrical/Plumbing contractors. \\n6. \\nThe Contractor shall strictly\
      \ adhere to the working drawings or detail drawings and written \\ninstructions of the Engineer. \\n \\nTECHNICAL\
      \ SPECIFICATIONS  \\n \\n1.  \\nMATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP: \\nThe whole of the materials used on the work\
      \ \\nshall be new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants \\nshall\
      \ be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best, \\nmost workman\
      \ like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the \\nEngineer. \\n \\nThe workmanship\
      \ is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall \\ntake all steps immediately to\
      \ make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must \\nbe made of a special tradesman in all aspects\
      \ of the work and allowances must be made \\nin the rates for the same. \\n \\n \\nContractor shall maintain\
      \ uniform quality and consistency in workmanship throughout the \\nexecution of the work.  \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"180\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\

- kind: parameter
  text: The order of precedence for rehabilitation and restoration standards and technical specifications gives
    top priority to provisions in the Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical Specifications, followed by
    Technical Specifications, KPWD Specifications, MORTH Specifications, and Standard Codes of Practice respectively.

- kind: summary
  text: Technical specifications mandate that all materials used in rehabilitation and restoration must be new and
    of the best quality, complying with Indian Standards, with weigh batching or approved concrete batch mix plants
    used for concrete production where cement is measured by weight (1 m3 equals 1440 kg or a 50 kg bag equals 35
    liters) and coarse aggregate size is specified as 20 mm and down unless otherwise stated.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jb5873f13c1466408
  relation_type: none

    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 34,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"181\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n        Materials shall be of the best approved quality obtainable\
      \ and they shall comply with the\\n       respective Indian Standard Specification.\\n        In case of non-availability\
      \ of materials in metric sizes, the nearest size in FPS units shall\\n      be provided with the prior approval\
      \ of the Engineer for which neither extra will be paid\\n       nor shall any rebate be recovered.\\n    \
      \        It shall be obligatory for the Contractor to furnish Certificate, if demanded by the Engineer,\\\
      n       from manufacturer or the material supplier that the work has been carried out by using\\n        \
      \ their material and as per their recommendation.\\n       Unless otherwise shown on the drawings or mentioned\
      \ in the schedule of quantities or\\n        special specifications the quality of materials, workmanship,\
      \ dimensions, etc., shall be as\\n        specified here in under.\\n         All equipment and facilities\
      \ for carrying out field tests on materials shall be provided\\n        without any extra cost.\\n\\n2.  \
      \  TESTING OF WORKS AND MATERIALS:              If required by the Engineer/Employer\\n       the Contractor\
      \ shall arrange to test materials, concrete cubes etc. and portions of the\\n      work in order to prove\
      \ their soundness and sufficiency at his own cost. If, after any such\\n        test and in the opinion of\
      \ the Engineer the materials or workmanship is found defective\\n       or unsound, the Contractor shall pull\
      \ down and re-execute the same at his own cost. All\\n       materials deemed defective in the opinion of\
      \ the Engineer shall be removed from the site\\n       immediately.\\n\\n3.    SAMPLES OF MATERIAL: The Contractor\
      \ shall deposit at the office of the Engineer\\n        for their approval samples of the materials proposed\
      \ to be used in the works, at least 15\\n      days before placing the order supply, and the bulk shall be\
      \ equal in every respect to the\\n      approved samples deposited.\\n\\n4.    STORAGE:   Safe, dry and proper\
      \ storage shall be provided for all materials, particularly\\n        for materials like cement. Adequate\
      \ storage should be made at least sufficient for 1 month\\n       during active construction.\\n      The\
      \ Contractor shall procure necessary guarantees from the manufactures and furnish\\n       the same to the\
      \ Engineer.\\n\\n5.    SPECIFICATIONS FOR CONCRETE WORKS\\n\\n      Concrete General\\n      Wherever grade\
      \ of concrete like M15, M20 etc. are mentioned, the concrete mix shall be\\n       designed by the contractor\
      \ and approval of Engineer for the same shall be obtained.\\n      Weigh batching shall be adopted for producing\
      \ the concrete. Obtaining of concrete from\\n       concrete batch mix plant will be permitted. Contractor\
      \ shall obtain approval of Engineer\\n         for each such batching plant. Proportion of ordinary cement\
      \ concrete will be expressed\\n      as l:3:6, 1:2:4, 1:1.5:3 etc. in the specification. The first figure\
      \ will be the quantity of\\n      cement by volume, the second figure will be dry coarse sand (fine aggregate)\
      \ by volume\\n      and the third figure will be the quantity of course aggregate by volume. Cement shall\
      \ be\\n      measured by weight. The weight is to be derived on the basis that 1 cubic meter of\\n      cement\
      \ will weigh 1440 kgs., or one full bag of 50 kgs. will be assumed to be 35 ltrs.\\n      when the sand is\
      \ wet or moist suitable correction for bulking is to be given while\\n        proportioning. Engineer may\
      \ allow measuring cement by volume.\\n       Unless otherwise specified the rates for all RCC will be exclusive\
      \ or reinforcements.\\n       Reinforcements will be paid for separate1y.\\n       Unless, otherwise stated,\
      \ for all RCC work the size of course aggregate will be 20 mm\\n      and down size.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 31 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\

      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        71.99947357177734,\n        74.16320037841797,\n        539.2294921875,\n        767.55810546875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \" \\nMaterials shall be of the best approved quality\
      \ obtainable and they shall comply with the \\nrespective Indian Standard Specification. \\n \\nIn case of\
      \ non-availability of materials in metric sizes, the nearest size in FPS units shall \\nbe provided with the\
      \ prior approval of the Engineer for which neither extra will be paid \\nnor shall any rebate be recovered.\
      \ \\n \\nIt shall be obligatory for the Contractor to furnish Certificate, if demanded by the Engineer, \\\
      nfrom manufacturer or the material supplier that the work has been carried out by using \\ntheir material\
      \ and as per their recommendation.  \\n \\nUnless otherwise shown on the drawings or mentioned in the schedule\
      \ of quantities or \\nspecial specifications the quality of materials, workmanship, dimensions, etc., shall\
      \ be as \\nspecified here in under. \\n \\nAll equipment and facilities for carrying out field tests on materials\
      \ shall be provided \\nwithout any extra cost. \\n \\n2.  \\nTESTING OF WORKS AND MATERIALS:  \\nIf required\
      \ by the Engineer/Employer \\nthe Contractor shall arrange to test materials, concrete cubes etc. and portions\
      \ of the \\nwork in order to prove their soundness and sufficiency at his own cost. If, after any such \\\
      ntest and in the opinion of the Engineer the materials or workmanship is found defective \\nor unsound, the\
      \ Contractor shall pull down and re-execute the same at his own cost. All \\nmaterials deemed defective in\
      \ the opinion of the Engineer shall be removed from the site \\nimmediately. \\n \\n3.  \\nSAMPLES OF MATERIAL:\
      \ The Contractor shall deposit at the office of the Engineer \\nfor their approval samples of the materials\
      \ proposed to be used in the works, at least 15 \\ndays before placing the order supply, and the bulk shall\
      \ be equal in every respect to the \\napproved samples deposited. \\n \\n4.  \\nSTORAGE: \\nSafe, dry and\
      \ proper storage shall be provided for all materials, particularly \\nfor materials like cement. Adequate\
      \ storage should be made at least sufficient for 1 month \\nduring active construction. \\n \\nThe Contractor\
      \ shall procure necessary guarantees from the manufactures and furnish \\nthe same to the Engineer. \\n \\\
      n5.  \\nSPECIFICATIONS FOR CONCRETE WORKS \\n \\n \\n \\n \\nConcrete General \\n \\nWherever grade of concrete\
      \ like M15, M20 etc. are mentioned, the concrete mix shall be \\ndesigned by the contractor and approval of\
      \ Engineer for the same shall be obtained. \\nWeigh batching shall be adopted for producing the concrete.\
      \ Obtaining of concrete from \\nconcrete batch mix plant will be permitted. Contractor shall obtain approval\
      \ of Engineer \\nfor each such batching plant. Proportion of ordinary cement concrete will be expressed \\\
      nas l:3:6, 1:2:4, 1:1.5:3 etc. in the specification. The first figure will be the quantity of \\ncement by\
      \ volume, the second figure will be dry coarse sand (fine aggregate) by volume \\nand the third figure will\
      \ be the quantity of course aggregate by volume. Cement shall be \\nmeasured by weight. The weight is to be\
      \ derived on the basis that 1 cubic meter of \\ncement will weigh 1440 kgs., or one full bag of 50 kgs. will\
      \ be assumed to be 35 ltrs. \\nwhen the sand is wet or moist suitable correction for bulking is to be given\
      \ while \\nproportioning. Engineer may allow measuring cement by volume.  \\n \\nUnless otherwise specified\
      \ the rates for all RCC will be exclusive or reinforcements. \\nReinforcements will be paid for separate1y.\
      \ \\n \\nUnless, otherwise stated, for all RCC work the size of course aggregate will be 20 mm \\nand down\
      \ size. \\n \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n\
      \        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"181\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Fresh concrete must be conveyed from mixing to final deposit within not more than 30 minutes to prevent
    segregation, protected against drying wind and direct sun, and cured for not less than 10 days.
  evidence_ids:
  - J508fec85b57149d9
  - J2707c9c8171b144c

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"9\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"9.\
      \   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\\n       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\\n       of any of the ingredients.\
      \ If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\\n      remixed before being placed. Normally\
      \ not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\\n      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    }\n  ],\n\
      \  \"heading_line\": \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\
      ,\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"       of any of the ingredients. If segregation\
      \ does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 48,\n      \"text\": \"      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse\
      \ between\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 50,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      TRANSPORTING\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\\n      sets\
      \ and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected\\n      from drying\
      \ wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\n       of any kind and generally\
      \ fit for drinking.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall\
      \ be protected\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"       of any kind\
      \ and generally fit for drinking.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 55,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      CURING\"\n}"

  text: Volume 2 Part 4B details the technical specifications and execution standards for concrete works, formwork,
    reinforcement, and general restoration standards for safety reasons under BMRCL Tender Package P1.

    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5
    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation

    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b

    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /source_page_context/4

    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 37,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"184\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n             effected by scrubbing the concrete surface\
      \ with wire and with bristle brushes and washing\\n          down to expose clearly the aggregate. However,\
      \ care shall be taken to avoid dislodgement\\n              of particles of aggregate.\\n                \
      \       If concrete has been allowed to harden excessively the surface shall be chipped over its\\n      \
      \      whole surface to a depth of at least 10 mm and thereafter thoroughly washed. Before\\n            \
      \  fresh concrete is added on the construction joints, the surface of the old concrete shall\\n          \
      \ be thoroughly wetted and covered with a thin layer of cement mortar 1:2.\\n\\n      12.   VERTICAL JOINTS:\
      \ At the end of any day's work or run of concrete, the concrete shall\\n           be finished of against\
      \ temporary shutter stop which shall be vertical and securely fixed.\\n             This stop shall be removed\
      \ as soon as the weather permits.\\n\\n      13.   STRENGTH OF ORDINARY CONCRETE:       The contractors have\
      \ to see that\\n             proper materials are used and the proportion and the correct water cement ratio\
      \ just\\n                sufficient for the workability are maintained to see that the following minimum strength\
      \ of\\n             concrete are obtained.\\n           To verify this, test cubes from the concrete used\
      \ should be made and tested. The\\n             frequency of testing and the acceptability criteria will be\
      \ according to IS 456 and IS 516.\\n\\n     14   CONCRETE FORMWORK AND CENTERING:\\n\\n      14.1   General:\\\
      n           Form work shall be rigidly constructed of 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets\\n   \
      \             for column, pedestals, beams, walls and fascia and steel or plywood for roof slab,\\n      \
      \       canopies, floors, cover slabs, retaining walls, etc. Shuttering faces in contact with the\\n     \
      \        concrete shall be free from adhering grout, projecting nails, splits or other defects that\\n   \
      \          are likely to mar the concrete surface. The shuttering shall be erected on battens, beams\\n  \
      \         and steel props properly cross-braced so as to make the form work rigid.\\n           Form work\
      \ shall be erected true to line and levels and to the required shapes as called\\n                for and\
      \ shall be able to carry without deformation the full weight of wet concrete and live\\n              loads.\
      \ It shall also withstand the effect of vibration without deflection, bulging, distortion\\n             \
      \ or loosening of its component parts. Details of centering and form work shall be subject\\n            \
      \   to the approval of the Engineer. The completed form work shall also be subject to the\\n             approval\
      \ by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor shall be\\n              responsible for sufficiency\
      \ and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds.\\n                 All joints shall be sufficiently\
      \ tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be\\n             provided at all corners wherever\
      \ called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer.\\n            Openings may .be given with the\
      \ approval of Engineer at convenient places for washing\\n           down all the rubbish. These are to be\
      \ closed before concreting.\\n\\n      14.2  Surface Treatment of Shuttering:  The surfaces of shuttering\
      \ coming into contact with\\n             concrete shall be plastic coated of approved make. The shuttering\
      \ shall be thoroughly\\n            cleaned and coated before each use.\\n      14.3  Removal of Form Work:\
      \     All formwork shall be removed without shock or vibration\\n           and shall be eased off carefully\
      \ in order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\\n           Forms shall not be disturbed\
      \ until, concrete had adequately hardened to take up its own\\n             weight and superimposed load coming\
      \ on it and in no circumstance shall forms be struck\\n                 until the concrete reaches strength\
      \ of at least twice the stress to which the concrete may\\n             subjected at the time of striking.\
      \ The side forms shall be so fixed that while removing\\n           them the supporting forms & posts are\
      \ not disturbed.\\n      14.4  Surface Finish of Concrete:\\n            Only cement mortar rendering of maximum\
      \ 6 mm thickness may be permitted as finishing\\n               to concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall\
      \ not be patched but shall be repaired by\\n            removal and replacement of the member as directed\
      \ by the Engineer.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026\
      \           Section Page 34 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n\
      \        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157              Employer Requirement,\
      \ Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n\
      \        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section\
      \ Page 34 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n\
      \        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99951171875,\n        74.16320037841797,\n\
      \        539.2294921875,\n        754.9581298828125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"effected by scrubbing the concrete surface with wire and with bristle brushes and washing \\ndown to expose\
      \ clearly the aggregate. However, care shall be taken to avoid dislodgement \\nof particles of aggregate.\
      \ \\n \\nIf concrete has been allowed to harden excessively the surface shall be chipped over its \\nwhole\
      \ surface to a depth of at least 10 mm and thereafter thoroughly washed. Before \\nfresh concrete is added\
      \ on the construction joints, the surface of the old concrete shall \\nbe thoroughly wetted and covered with\
      \ a thin layer of cement mortar 1:2. \\n \\n12.  \\n VERTICAL JOINTS: At the end of any day's work or run\
      \ of concrete, the concrete shall \\nbe finished of against temporary shutter stop which shall be vertical\
      \ and securely fixed. \\nThis stop shall be removed as soon as the weather permits. \\n \\n13.  \\nSTRENGTH\
      \ OF ORDINARY CONCRETE:  \\nThe contractors have to see that \\nproper materials are used and the proportion\
      \ and the correct water cement ratio just \\nsufficient for the workability are maintained to see that the\
      \ following minimum strength of \\nconcrete are obtained. \\n \\nTo verify this, test cubes from the concrete\
      \ used should be made and tested. The \\nfrequency of testing and the acceptability criteria will be according\
      \ to IS 456 and IS 516. \\n \\n14 \\n CONCRETE FORMWORK AND CENTERING: \\n \\n14.1 \\n General: \\n \\nForm\
      \ work shall be rigidly constructed of 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets \\nfor column, pedestals,\
      \ beams, walls and fascia and steel or plywood for roof slab, \\ncanopies, floors, cover slabs, retaining\
      \ walls, etc. Shuttering faces in contact with the \\nconcrete shall be free from adhering grout, projecting\
      \ nails, splits or other defects that \\nare likely to mar the concrete surface. The shuttering shall be erected\
      \ on battens, beams \\nand steel props properly cross-braced so as to make the form work rigid. \\n \\nForm\
      \ work shall be erected true to line and levels and to the required shapes as called \\nfor and shall be able\
      \ to carry without deformation the full weight of wet concrete and live \\nloads. It shall also withstand\
      \ the effect of vibration without deflection, bulging, distortion \\nor loosening of its component parts.\
      \ Details of centering and form work shall be subject \\nto the approval of the Engineer. The completed form\
      \ work shall also be subject to the \\napproval by the Engineer before placement of reinforcement. Contractor\
      \ shall be \\nresponsible for sufficiency and adequacy of all form work centering and moulds. \\n \\nAll joints\
      \ shall be sufficiently tight to prevent leakage of grout. Chamfer fillets shall be \\nprovided at all corners\
      \ wherever called for on the drawings or as directed by the Engineer. \\n \\nOpenings may .be given with the\
      \ approval of Engineer at convenient places for washing \\ndown all the rubbish. These are to be closed before\
      \ concreting. \\n \\n14.2    Surface Treatment of Shuttering: The surfaces of shuttering coming into contact\
      \ with \\nconcrete shall be plastic coated of approved make. The shuttering shall be thoroughly \\ncleaned\
      \ and coated before each use. \\n14.3    Removal of Form Work: \\nAll formwork shall be removed without shock\
      \ or vibration \\nand shall be eased off carefully in order to allow the structure to take up its load gradually.\
      \ \\n \\nForms shall not be disturbed until, concrete had adequately hardened to take up its own \\nweight\
      \ and superimposed load coming on it and in no circumstance shall forms be struck \\nuntil the concrete reaches\
      \ strength of at least twice the stress to which the concrete may \\nsubjected at the time of striking. The\
      \ side forms shall be so fixed that while removing \\nthem the supporting forms & posts are not disturbed.\
      \ \\n14.4    Surface Finish of Concrete: \\nOnly cement mortar rendering of maximum 6 mm thickness may be\
      \ permitted as finishing \\nto concrete surface. Imperfect surfaces shall not be patched but shall be repaired\
      \ by \\nremoval and replacement of the member as directed by the Engineer. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"184\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

  - id: J0fa404cc10eb35ce
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5
    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation

    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b

    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /source_page_context/5
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 38,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"185\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n            If centering and shuttering is to be paid for\
      \ separately, measurement will be taken\\n       according to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one\
      \ square meter.\\n         All aggregate are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates\
      \ will be\\n       tested as frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the\
      \ same\\n      as adopted in the mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the\\n       purpose.\\\
      n       Concrete shall be weighed batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\\n      checked\
      \ with standard weights periodically. The conversions of weights to volume will be\\n       allowed by the\
      \ Engineer under special circumstances. Despite the design for several\\n       mixes the following quantities\
      \ of cement are the minimum to be used for the different\\n       grades of the concrete:-\\n      Cement\
      \ concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:3:6)                 -\
      \ 4.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags per cum.\\n     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per\
      \ cum.\\n     RCC              (1:1:2)            -10.80 Bags per cum\\n\\n14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\\n\
      \       Immediately on removal of form work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined by\\n       the\
      \ contractor and any bone combs or other imperfections shall be brought to the notice\\n        of the Engineer.\
      \ The acceptability or otherwise of such defective concrete shall be at the\\n       sole discretion of the\
      \ Engineer who may direct the contractor to repair the defective work\\n        or ask for demolition and\
      \ replacement of such defective work at the risk and cost of the\\n        contractor.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 35 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 35 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        72.0003662109375,\n        74.16320037841797,\n        539.2297973632812,\n        400.7181701660156\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately,\
      \ measurement will be taken \\naccording to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter. \\\
      n \\nAll aggregate are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates will be \\ntested as\
      \ frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the same \\nas adopted in the\
      \ mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the \\npurpose. \\n \\nConcrete shall be weighed\
      \ batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be \\nchecked with standard weights periodically.\
      \ The conversions of weights to volume will be \\nallowed by the Engineer under special circumstances. Despite\
      \ the design for several \\nmixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used for the different\
      \ \\ngrades of the concrete:- \\n \\nCement concrete (1:4:8)  \\n \\n- 3.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\nCement concrete\
      \ (1:3:6)  \\n \\n- 4.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\nCement concrete (1:2:4)  \\n \\n- 6.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\\
      nCement concrete (1:1.5:3)  \\n \\n- 8.00 Bags per cum.  \\n \\n \\nRCC                    (1:1.5:3)  \\n\
      \ \\n- 8.00 Bags per cum. \\n \\nRCC                    (1:1:2)  \\n \\n-10.80 Bags per cum \\n \\n14.5. \
      \  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE: \\nImmediately on removal of form work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined\
      \ by \\nthe contractor and any bone combs or other imperfections shall be brought to the notice \\nof the\
      \ Engineer. The acceptability or otherwise of such defective concrete shall be at the \\nsole discretion of\
      \ the Engineer who may direct the contractor to repair the defective work \\nor ask for demolition and replacement\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\\n      sets\
      \ and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected\\n      from drying\
      \ wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\n       of any kind and generally\
      \ fit for drinking.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall\
      \ be protected\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"       of any kind\
      \ and generally fit for drinking.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 55,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      CURING\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 33,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"180\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n    PART – B: SPECIFICATIONS FOR REHABILITATION AND\
      \ RESTORATION WORKS\\n\\n      Restoration works for safety reasons.\\n       1.    The BMRCL may appoint\
      \ consultant/Engineer to conduct Building Condition Study (BCS)\\n          and suggest sequence and methodology\
      \ of demolition including remedial measures\\n             required to be undertaken prior to demolition and/or\
      \ during demolition and/or post\\n             demolition.\\n                    It is anticipated that, certain\
      \ structures will be required to be constructed for safety and\\n              functional reasons as a result\
      \ of Building Condition Study. The structures anticipated are\\n               front walls, doors, windows,\
      \ beams and columns etc.\\n          The BMRCL has identified few items which are to be executed in this contract.\
      \ These\\n            items  shall be as per KPWD Common Schedule  of Rates  for the year 2023-24.\\n    \
      \       Measurements will be recorded for only those buildings completed under the supervision\\n        \
      \      of consultant/Engineer appointed for Building Condition Study.\\n       2.      All Standards, Technical\
      \ Specifications and Codes of Practice referred to shall be latest\\n             editions including all applicable\
      \ official amendments and revisions. The contractor shall\\n         make available at site all relevant KPWD\
      \ Specifications and Indian Standard Codes of\\n             practice as applicable.\\n              In case\
      \ of discrepancy among Standard codes of practice, Technical Specifications and\\n             provisions\
      \ in Sub clauses in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n              Specifications’,\
      \ the order of precedence will be as below:\\n\\n                               i)  Provision in this ‘Employer\
      \ Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical\\n                    Specifications’\\n                        \
      \         ii)  Technical Specifications\\n                                  iii) KPWD Specifications\\n  \
      \                  v) MORTH Specifications\\n                           vii) Standard Codes of Practice\\\
      n\\n       3.    The utilities which are not to be diverted but require supporting, proper supporting be done\\\
      n           so that they are not damaged along their branches. All Precautions shall be taken while\\n   \
      \         handling the utilities. The Contractor shall be liable for any damages in this behalf.\\n      \
      \ 4.    Before carrying out the work at site, necessary permissions as required from various local\\n    \
      \        agencies / authorities shall be required to be obtained by the contractor. The Employer\\n      \
      \        shall assist only by way of issue of necessary support letters.\\n       5.    The work has to be\
      \ executed carefully without damaging the existing surface/over\\n            ground/underground utilities.\
      \ The contractor shall execute electrical and plumbing works\\n            through Licensed Electrical/Plumbing\
      \ contractors.\\n       6.    The Contractor shall strictly adhere to the working drawings or detail drawings\
      \ and written\\n              instructions of the Engineer.\\n\\n     TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS\\n\\n     \
      \  1.    MATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP:     The whole of the materials used on the work\\n               shall\
      \ be new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants\\n              \
      \ shall be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best,\\n       \
      \    most workman like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the\\n             Engineer.\\\
      n           The workmanship is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall\\n  \
      \           take all steps immediately to make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must\\n \
      \          be made of a special tradesman in all aspects of the work and allowances must be made\\n      \
      \          in the rates for the same.\\n\\n              Contractor shall maintain uniform quality and consistency\
      \ in workmanship throughout the\\n             execution of the work.\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail\
      \ Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 30 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\

      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 30 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\

      \        71.99996948242188,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        501.6673583984375,\n        84.39871978759766\n\

      \ WORKS \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00061798095703,\n        86.64318084716797,\n\

      : \" \\nRestoration works for safety reasons. \\n1. \\nThe BMRCL may appoint consultant/Engineer to conduct\
      \ Building Condition Study (BCS) \\nand suggest sequence and methodology of demolition including remedial\
      \ measures \\nrequired to be undertaken prior to demolition and/or during demolition and/or post \\ndemolition.\
      \   \\nIt is anticipated that, certain structures will be required to be constructed for safety and \\n\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        100.80119323730469,\n        175.32305908203125,\n        539.2283935546875,\n\
      \        210.9984588623047\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"functional reasons as\
      \ a result of Building Condition Study.  The structures anticipated are \\nfront walls, doors, windows, beams\
      \ and columns etc. \\nThe BMRCL has identified few items which are to be executed in this contract.  These\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00117492675781,\n        213.242919921875,\n       \
      \ 539.230712890625,\n        350.19818115234375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"\
      items shall be as per KPWD Common Schedule of Rates for the year 2023-24. \\nMeasurements will be recorded\
      \ for only those buildings completed under the supervision \\nof consultant/Engineer appointed for Building\
      \ Condition Study.  \\n2.  \\nAll Standards, Technical Specifications and Codes of Practice referred to shall\
      \ be latest \\neditions including all applicable official amendments and revisions. The contractor shall \\\
      nmake available at site all relevant KPWD Specifications and Indian Standard Codes of \\npractice as applicable.\
      \ \\n \\nIn case of discrepancy among Standard codes of practice, Technical Specifications and \\nprovisions\
      \ in Sub clauses in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical \\nSpecifications’, the order of\
      \ precedence will be as below: \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00119018554688,\n \
      \       352.44268798828125,\n        539.2310180664062,\n        767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \" \\ni)    Provision in this ‘Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical \\nSpecifications’\
      \ \\n \\nii)   Technical Specifications \\n \\niii)  KPWD Specifications \\n \\nv)   MORTH Specifications\
      \ \\n \\nvii) Standard Codes of Practice \\n \\n3.  \\nThe utilities which are not to be diverted but require\
      \ supporting, proper supporting be done \\nso that they are not damaged along their branches. All Precautions\
      \ shall be taken while \\nhandling the utilities.   The Contractor shall be liable for any damages in this\
      \ behalf. \\n4. \\nBefore carrying out the work at site, necessary permissions as required from various local\
      \ \\nagencies / authorities shall be required to be obtained by the contractor.  The Employer \\nshall assist\
      \ only by way of issue of necessary support letters.  \\n5. \\nThe work has to be executed carefully without\
      \ damaging the existing surface/over \\nground/underground utilities. The contractor shall execute electrical\
      \ and plumbing works \\nthrough Licensed Electrical/Plumbing contractors. \\n6. \\nThe Contractor shall strictly\
      \ adhere to the working drawings or detail drawings and written \\ninstructions of the Engineer. \\n \\nTECHNICAL\
      \ SPECIFICATIONS  \\n \\n1.  \\nMATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP: \\nThe whole of the materials used on the work\
      \ \\nshall be new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants \\nshall\
      \ be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best, \\nmost workman\
      \ like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the \\nEngineer. \\n \\nThe workmanship\
      \ is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall \\ntake all steps immediately to\
      \ make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must \\nbe made of a special tradesman in all aspects\
      \ of the work and allowances must be made \\nin the rates for the same. \\n \\n \\nContractor shall maintain\
      \ uniform quality and consistency in workmanship throughout the \\nexecution of the work.  \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"180\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"12\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"\
      12.   VERTICAL JOINTS: At the end of any day's work or run of concrete, the concrete shall\\n           be\
      \ finished of against temporary shutter stop which shall be vertical and securely fixed.\\n             This\
      \ stop shall be removed as soon as the weather permits.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"      12.  \
      \ VERTICAL JOINTS: At the end of any day's work or run of concrete, the concrete shall\",\n  \"retained_lines\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"      12.   VERTICAL\
      \ JOINTS: At the end of any day's work or run of concrete, the concrete shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"           be finished of against temporary shutter\
      \ stop which shall be vertical and securely fixed.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"             This stop shall be removed as soon as the weather permits.\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 37,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"\
      review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"VERTICAL JOINTS\"\n}"

    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"9\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"9.\
      \   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\\n       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\\n       of any of the ingredients.\
      \ If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\\n      remixed before being placed. Normally\
      \ not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\\n      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    }\n  ],\n\
      \  \"heading_line\": \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\
      ,\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"       of final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"       of any of the ingredients. If segregation\
      \ does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 48,\n      \"text\": \"      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse\
      \ between\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \      mixing and consolidation in position.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 50,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      TRANSPORTING\"\n}"

    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\\n        yield\
      \ strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966, Fe-500 or\\n      as called for on\
      \ the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\\n       mild steel wire mesh\
      \ conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\\n        materials, mill scale, loose\
      \ rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\\n       schedules for approval of the Engineer\
      \ prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall\\n        indicate the accurate dimensions and bending\
      \ of bars as called for on the structural\\n       drawings. Fabrication shall be accurately done to the dimensions,\
      \ spacing and ensuring\\n      minimum cover as called for on structural drawings. All steel shall be rigidly\
      \ held in place\\n       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape,\
      \ M.S.\\n       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order to ensure accurate positioning  of\\n     \
      \  reinforcement.\\n         All reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and\
      \ firmly wired at\\n        least one day, prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor\
      \ shall also\\n       ensure that all conduits and inserts are in position before placing concrete.\\n   \
      \   Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with a radius of not\\n        less\
      \ than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at least four\\n       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\\n       diameters plus an extension\
      \ of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\\n      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times\
      \ the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\n      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints\
      \ at the same location. Only where\\n        specifically shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have\
      \ welded joints. Rate quoted\\n         for steel reinforcement work shall be deemed to include the cost of\
      \ such welding\\n       wherever specified.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel\
      \ reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement\

      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"        yield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966,\
      \ Fe-500 or\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \      as called for on the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\"\n    },\n\

      \ These shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\

      \ shall be accurately done to the dimensions, spacing and ensuring\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n\

      \ All steel shall be rigidly held in place\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\

      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"       diameters plus an extension of 12 bar diameters.\
      \ Unless otherwise shown or specified\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 25,\n      \"text\": \"      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times the diameter of bar. Not more than\
      \ 33%. (Thirty-\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints at the same location. Only where\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"        specifically\
      \ shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have welded joints. Rate quoted\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"         for steel reinforcement work shall be\
      \ deemed to include the cost of such welding\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 29,\n      \"text\": \"       wherever specified.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 30,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"\
      REINFORCEMENT\"\n}"

    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5

    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /source_page_context/2
