    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 35,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"182\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n       6.   CEMENT CONCRETE PLAIN AND REINFORCED:\\\
      n\\n            A) Cement:  Cement shall comply in every respect with the requirements of the latest\\n  \
      \               publication of IS: 8112 and unless otherwise specified, ordinary Portland cement shall\\n\
      \             be used. No other make of cement but that approved by the Engineer will be allowed\\n      \
      \       on works and the source of supply not be changed without approval of the Engineer in\\n          \
      \         writing. Test certificates to show that the cement used fully complies with the relevant\\n    \
      \            IS specifications shall be submitted to the Engineer and not withstanding this, the\\n      \
      \         Engineer may at their discretion order that the cement brought to site and which they\\n       \
      \     may consider damaged or of doubtful quality for any reasons whatsoever shall be re-\\n             \
      \   tested in an approved testing laboratory and fresh certificate of its soundness shall be\\n          \
      \     produced. Cement ordered for retesting shall not be used for any work pending results\\n           \
      \       of retest.\\n             Cement shall be stored and neatly packed in piles not exceeding 10 bags\
      \ high in\\n               weather proof sheds with raised wooden plank flooring to prevent deterioration\
      \ by\\n             dampness or intrusion of foreign matter. It shall be stored in such a way as to allow\\\
      n                the removal and use of cement in chronological order of receipt, i.e. the first received\\\
      n               being first used. Cement deteriorated and/or clotted shall not be used on work but shall\\\
      n             be removed at once from the site.\\n\\n           B) Fine Aggregates: Sand shall conform to\
      \ IS: 383. It shall pass through 1.5 sieve 4.75\\n         mm (3/16 B.S) test sieve leaving a residue not\
      \ more than 5%. It shall be from a natural\\n               source or crushed stone screedings.  It shall\
      \ be washed  if directed to reduce the\\n               percentage of deleterious substances to acceptable\
      \ limits. Sand shall not contain any\\n                 trace of salt and sand containing any trace of salt\
      \ shall be rejected.\\n             The fine aggregate for concrete shall be graded within limits as specified\
      \ in IS: 383\\n             and the fineness modules shall range between 2.60 to 3.20. The fine aggregate\
      \ shall\\n               stacked carefully on a clean hard dry surface so that  it will not get mixed up with\\\
      n                 deleterious foreign materials. If such a surface is not available a platform of planks or\\\
      n                corrugated sheets or brick floor or concrete floor shall be prepared. Sand shall be\\n  \
      \           added in the desired proportion as required for the strength specified, with suitable\\n     \
      \            correction for bulking.\\n\\n           C) Coarse Aggregates:    Coarse aggregate shall conform\
      \ to IS: 383. It shall consist\\n                  of crushed or broken stone, 95% of which shall, be retained\
      \ on 4.75 mm is test sieve.\\n                          It shall be obtained from crushed granite, trap, basalt\
      \ or similar approved stones from\\n              approved quarry. Coarse aggregate shall be chemically inert\
      \ when mixed with cement\\n           & shall be angular in shape and free from soft friable thin porous laminated\
      \ or flaky\\n                pieces. It shall be free from dust and other foreign matter. Gravel/shingle of\
      \ desired\\n                grading may be permitted as a substitute in part or full in plain cement concrete\
      \ if the\\n               Engineer are otherwise satisfied about the quality of aggregate.\\n\\n         \
      \  D) Water:    Water used for mixing concrete shall be fresh, clean, free from oil, salts,\\n           \
      \      acids, alkali and shall be in accordance with the clause 4.3 of IS 456 - 1978. The\\n             \
      \    contractor shall produce test results for approval by the Engineer for the mixing of\\n             \
      \  water proposed to be used on the job, before casting any concrete.\\n\\n            E) Mixing Of Concrete:\
      \    MACHINE  MIXED:  Aggregates  shall  be  accurately\\n             measured out in boxes and mixed dry\
      \ along with required cement, water shall then be\\n             added in measured quantity and mixing shall\
      \ be continued until there is uniform\\n                  distribution of the materials and the mass is uniform\
      \ in color and consistency but in no\\n              case shall the mixing be done for less than 2 minutes.\
      \ Only hopper loading mixer shall\\n             be used.\\n\\n          7.  CONSISTENCY:\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 32 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 32 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        71.99996948242188,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        364.74920654296875,\n        96.99869537353516\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"6. \\nCEMENT CONCRETE PLAIN AND REINFORCED: \\n \\\
      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        103.559814453125,\n        99.36315155029297,\n        539.2276611328125,\n\
      \        337.3581237792969\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"A) Cement: \\nCement shall\
      \ comply in every respect with the requirements of the latest \\npublication of IS: 8112 and unless otherwise\
      \ specified, ordinary Portland cement shall \\nbe used. No other make of cement but that approved by the Engineer\
      \ will be allowed \\non works and the source of supply not be changed without approval of the Engineer in\
      \ \\nwriting. Test certificates to show that the cement used fully complies with the relevant \\nIS specifications\
      \ shall be submitted to the Engineer and not withstanding this, the \\nEngineer may at their discretion order\
      \ that the cement brought to site and which they \\nmay consider damaged or of doubtful quality for any reasons\
      \ whatsoever shall be re-\\ntested in an approved testing laboratory and fresh certificate of its soundness\
      \ shall be \\nproduced. Cement ordered for retesting shall not be used for any work pending results \\nof\
      \ retest. \\n \\nCement shall be stored and neatly packed in piles not exceeding 10 bags high in \\nweather\
      \ proof sheds with raised wooden plank flooring to prevent deterioration by \\ndampness or intrusion of foreign\
      \ matter. It shall be stored in such a way as to allow \\nthe removal and use of cement in chronological order\
      \ of receipt, i.e. the first received \\nbeing first used. Cement deteriorated and/or clotted shall not be\
      \ used on work but shall \\nbe removed at once from the site.  \\n \\nB) Fine Aggregates: Sand shall conform\
      \ to IS: 383. It shall pass through 1.5 sieve 4.75 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00016021728516,\n\
      \        339.8426208496094,\n        539.229736328125,\n        754.9580688476562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"mm (3/16 B.S) test sieve leaving a residue not more than 5%. It shall be from a natural\
      \ \\nsource or crushed stone screedings. It shall be washed if directed to reduce the \\npercentage of deleterious\
      \ substances to acceptable limits. Sand shall not contain any \\ntrace of salt and sand containing any trace\
      \ of salt shall be rejected.  \\n \\nThe fine aggregate for concrete shall be graded within limits as specified\
      \ in IS: 383 \\nand the fineness modules shall range between 2.60 to 3.20. The fine aggregate shall \\nstacked\
      \ carefully on a clean hard dry surface so that it will not get mixed up with \\ndeleterious foreign materials.\
      \ If such a surface is not available a platform of planks or \\ncorrugated sheets or brick floor or concrete\
      \ floor shall be prepared. Sand shall be \\nadded in the desired proportion as required for the strength specified,\
      \ with suitable \\ncorrection for bulking. \\n \\nC)  Coarse Aggregates: \\nCoarse aggregate shall conform\
      \ to IS: 383. It shall consist \\nof crushed or broken stone, 95% of which shall, be retained on 4.75 mm is\
      \ test sieve. \\nIt shall be obtained from crushed granite, trap, basalt or similar approved stones from \\\
      napproved quarry. Coarse aggregate shall be chemically inert when mixed with cement \\n& shall be angular\
      \ in shape and free from soft friable thin porous laminated or flaky \\npieces. It shall be free from dust\
      \ and other foreign matter. Gravel/shingle of desired \\ngrading may be permitted as a substitute in part\
      \ or full in plain cement concrete if the \\nEngineer are otherwise satisfied about the quality of aggregate.\
      \ \\n \\nD) Water: \\nWater used for mixing concrete shall be fresh, clean, free from oil, salts, \\nacids,\
      \ alkali and shall be in accordance with the clause 4.3 of IS 456 - 1978. The \\ncontractor shall produce\
      \ test results for approval by the Engineer for the mixing of \\nwater proposed to be used on the job, before\
      \ casting any concrete. \\n \\nE)  Mixing Of Concrete: \\nMACHINE MIXED: Aggregates shall be accurately \\\
      nmeasured out in boxes and mixed dry along with required cement, water shall then be \\nadded in measured\
      \ quantity and mixing shall be continued until there is uniform \\ndistribution of the materials and the mass\
      \ is uniform in color and consistency but in no \\ncase shall the mixing be done for less than 2 minutes.\
      \ Only hopper loading mixer shall \\nbe used. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.56014251708984,\n\
      \        757.08251953125,\n        194.22927856445312,\n        767.4380493164062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 6,\n      \"text\": \"7. \\nCONSISTENCY: \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n\
      \        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"182\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 40,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"187\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement shall be either\
      \ mild steel of tested quality high\\n        yield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS;\
      \ 1786--1966, Fe-500 or\\n      as called for on the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall\
      \ be of hard drawn\\n       mild steel wire mesh conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\\\
      n        materials, mill scale, loose rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\\n     \
      \  schedules for approval of the Engineer prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall\\n        indicate\
      \ the accurate dimensions and bending of bars as called for on the structural\\n       drawings. Fabrication\
      \ shall be accurately done to the dimensions, spacing and ensuring\\n      minimum cover as called for on\
      \ structural drawings. All steel shall be rigidly held in place\\n       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire.\
      \ Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape, M.S.\\n       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order\
      \ to ensure accurate positioning  of\\n       reinforcement.\\n         All reinforcing steel within the limit\
      \ of a day's polish shall be in place and firmly wired at\\n        least one day, prior to the date of pour\
      \ to permit inspection. The contractor shall also\\n       ensure that all conduits and inserts are in position\
      \ before placing concrete.\\n      Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with\
      \ a radius of not\\n        less than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at\
      \ least four\\n       bar diameters at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than\
      \ 4 bar\\n       diameters plus an extension of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\\n \
      \     on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\n      Three\
      \ Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints at the same location. Only where\\n        specifically shown\
      \ on the drawings, reinforcement shall have welded joints. Rate quoted\\n         for steel reinforcement\
      \ work shall be deemed to include the cost of such welding\\n       wherever specified.\\n\\n16.   STACKING\
      \ AND STORAGE:       Steel for reinforcement shall be stored in such a\\n     way as to prevent distorting\
      \ and corrosion. Bars of different classifications, sizes and\\n      lengths shall be stored separately to\
      \ facilitate issue in such sizes and lengths to cause to\\n     minimum wastage in cutting from standard length.\\\
      n\\n17.   EARTHWORK INCLUDING GENERAL FILLING, DEWATERING AND BACKFILLING:\\n      These Specifications cover\
      \ the general requirements for earthwork and related works in\\n        different types of soils and rocks.\
      \ The scope of work includes, but is not limited to,\\n        clearing of the site; general filling of site;\
      \ compaction of fill to required degree; excavation\\n      and backfilling works; works related to stabilizing\
      \ of sides of excavations; and necessary\\n       dewatering all within the intent of these specifications.\
      \ The scope shall include provision\\n        of access to different parts of the excavated areas, adequate\
      \ protective safety measures,\\n      and proper lighting and necessary signage arrangements.\\n       General\
      \ filling using approved material shall be done in areas as indicated in drawings\\n        or as directed\
      \ by the Engineer.\\n      The tenderer should acquaint him with the site for assessing the efforts necessary\
      \ for\\n       completing the work as per these specifications.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 37 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n  \
      \    \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n  \
      \      56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 37 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        71.99946594238281,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        539.2288818359375,\n        603.1581420898438\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"15.  \\nREINFORCEMENT: Steel reinforcement shall\
      \ be either mild steel of tested quality high \\nyield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to\
      \ IS; 1786--1966, Fe-500 or \\nas called for on the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be\
      \ of hard drawn \\nmild steel wire mesh conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious \\\
      nmaterials, mill scale, loose rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending \\nschedules for\
      \ approval of the Engineer prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall \\nindicate the accurate dimensions\
      \ and bending of bars as called for on the structural \\ndrawings. Fabrication shall be accurately done to\
      \ the dimensions, spacing and ensuring \\nminimum cover as called for on structural drawings. All steel shall\
      \ be rigidly held in place \\nwith 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape,\
      \ M.S. \\nchairs and spacer bars shall be used in order to ensure accurate positioning of \\nreinforcement.\
      \ \\n \\nAll reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and firmly wired at \\\
      nleast one day, prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor shall also \\nensure that all\
      \ conduits and inserts are in position before placing concrete. \\n \\nHooks where indicated shall be either\
      \ a complete semi-circular turn with a radius of not \\nless than four and not more than six bar's diameters,\
      \ plus an extension of at least four \\nbar diameters at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius\
      \ of not less than 4 bar \\ndiameters plus an extension of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\
      \ \\non the drawings, all lapped by 50 times the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\nThree Percent)\
      \ of the bars shall have lapped joints at the same location. Only where \\nspecifically shown on the drawings,\
      \ reinforcement shall have welded joints. Rate quoted \\nfor steel reinforcement work shall be deemed to include\
      \ the cost of such welding \\nwherever specified. \\n \\n16.  \\n STACKING AND STORAGE: \\nSteel for reinforcement\
      \ shall be stored in such a \\nway as to prevent distorting and corrosion. Bars of different classifications,\
      \ sizes and \\nlengths shall be stored separately to facilitate issue in such sizes and lengths to cause to\
      \ \\nminimum wastage in cutting from standard length. \\n \\n17.  \\nEARTHWORK INCLUDING GENERAL FILLING,\
      \ DEWATERING AND BACKFILLING: \\nThese Specifications cover the general requirements for earthwork and related\
      \ works in \\ndifferent types of soils and rocks. The scope of work includes, but is not limited to, \\nclearing\
      \ of the site; general filling of site; compaction of fill to required degree; excavation \\nand backfilling\
      \ works; works related to stabilizing of sides of excavations; and necessary \\ndewatering all within the\
      \ intent of these specifications. The scope shall include provision \\nof access to different parts of the\
      \ excavated areas, adequate protective safety measures, \\nand proper lighting and necessary signage arrangements.\
      \     \\nGeneral filling using approved material shall be done in areas as indicated in drawings \\nor as\
      \ directed by the Engineer.  \\nThe tenderer should acquaint him with the site for assessing the efforts necessary\
      \ for \\ncompleting the work as per these specifications. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n      \
      \  546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n \
      \     ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"187\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: 'Minimum cement contents for concrete grades are specified as follows: CC 1:4:8 at 3.40 bags/cum, CC 1:3:6
    at 4.40 bags/cum, CC 1:2:4 at 6.40 bags/cum, CC 1:1.5:3 at 8.00 bags/cum, RCC 1:1.5:3 at 8.00 bags/cum, and
    RCC 1:1:2 at 10.80 bags/cum.'

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 38,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"185\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n            If centering and shuttering is to be paid for\
      \ separately, measurement will be taken\\n       according to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one\
      \ square meter.\\n         All aggregate are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates\
      \ will be\\n       tested as frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the\
      \ same\\n      as adopted in the mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the\\n       purpose.\\\
      n       Concrete shall be weighed batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be\\n      checked\
      \ with standard weights periodically. The conversions of weights to volume will be\\n       allowed by the\
      \ Engineer under special circumstances. Despite the design for several\\n       mixes the following quantities\
      \ of cement are the minimum to be used for the different\\n       grades of the concrete:-\\n      Cement\
      \ concrete (1:4:8)                 - 3.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:3:6)                 -\
      \ 4.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete (1:2:4)                 - 6.40 Bags per cum.\\n      Cement concrete\
      \ (1:1.5:3)              - 8.00 Bags per cum.\\n     RCC             (1:1.5:3)               - 8.00 Bags per\
      \ cum.\\n     RCC              (1:1:2)            -10.80 Bags per cum\\n\\n14.5.  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE:\\n\
      \       Immediately on removal of form work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined by\\n       the\
      \ contractor and any bone combs or other imperfections shall be brought to the notice\\n        of the Engineer.\
      \ The acceptability or otherwise of such defective concrete shall be at the\\n       sole discretion of the\
      \ Engineer who may direct the contractor to repair the defective work\\n        or ask for demolition and\
      \ replacement of such defective work at the risk and cost of the\\n        contractor.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 35 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 35 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        72.0003662109375,\n        74.16320037841797,\n        539.2297973632812,\n        400.7181701660156\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"If centering and shuttering is to be paid for separately,\
      \ measurement will be taken \\naccording to IS 1200. The unit area for payment will be one square meter. \\\
      n \\nAll aggregate are to conform strictly to IS specification IS 383. The aggregates will be \\ntested as\
      \ frequently as directed by the Engineer to see that their specification is the same \\nas adopted in the\
      \ mix design. They must be stored on clean platform made for the \\npurpose. \\n \\nConcrete shall be weighed\
      \ batched. The dials of weigh batching unit to be used shall be \\nchecked with standard weights periodically.\
      \ The conversions of weights to volume will be \\nallowed by the Engineer under special circumstances. Despite\
      \ the design for several \\nmixes the following quantities of cement are the minimum to be used for the different\
      \ \\ngrades of the concrete:- \\n \\nCement concrete (1:4:8)  \\n \\n- 3.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\nCement concrete\
      \ (1:3:6)  \\n \\n- 4.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\nCement concrete (1:2:4)  \\n \\n- 6.40 Bags per cum. \\n \\\
      nCement concrete (1:1.5:3)  \\n \\n- 8.00 Bags per cum.  \\n \\n \\nRCC                    (1:1.5:3)  \\n\
      \ \\n- 8.00 Bags per cum. \\n \\nRCC                    (1:1:2)  \\n \\n-10.80 Bags per cum \\n \\n14.5. \
      \  DEFECTS IN CONCRETE: \\nImmediately on removal of form work, the surface of the concrete shall be examined\
      \ by \\nthe contractor and any bone combs or other imperfections shall be brought to the notice \\nof the\
      \ Engineer. The acceptability or otherwise of such defective concrete shall be at the \\nsole discretion of\
      \ the Engineer who may direct the contractor to repair the defective work \\nor ask for demolition and replacement\
      \ of such defective work at the risk and cost of the \\ncontractor. \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\

- kind: parameter
  text: Maximum total water including aggregate moisture per 50 kg of cement is restricted to 34 liters for 1:3:6
    mix, 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix, 30 liters for 1:1.5:3 mix, and 27 liters for 1:1:2 mix.

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"183\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n      Only sufficient water giving due allowance for the moisture\
      \ content of aggregate shall be\\n      added to the cement and aggregates during mixing to produce a mixture\
      \ of sufficient\\n        workability to enable  it to be well consolidated, to be worked into the corners\
      \ of the\\n       shuttering and around the reinforcements where there is reinforcement to give the\\n   \
      \    specified finish and to have specified strength.\\n       Normally for every 50 kg of cement total water\
      \ including moisture content of aggregate\\n       should not be more than 34 liters for 1:3:6 mix 32 liters\
      \ for 1:2:4 mix. 30 liters for 1:1½:3\\n      and 27 liters for 1:1:2.\\n            If  difficulty be experienced\
      \ in placing the concrete of specified mix and approved\\n       consistency between and below reinforcement\
      \ bars, in the bottom of beams and similar\\n        situations, the concrete shall have improved workability\
      \ by increasing the proportion of\\n       water and corresponding additional quantity of cement and using\
      \ aggregates of smaller\\n        size than specified as directed by Engineer for which no extra be paid.\\\
      n      The consistency shall be determined by making trial mixtures with dried aggregates, or\\n      when\
      \ so instructed by test of laboratory made test cubes under the direction of Engineer.\\n       Consistency\
      \ may be measured by slump test using a standard cone or the Engineer may\\n        direct the use of any\
      \ other means of testing the consistency.\\n            If the apparatus used for the slump test is a standard\
      \ cone when filled. It shall be raised\\n         vertically clear of the concrete and the measurement of\
      \ the slump shall be 300 mm minus\\n       the height of the slumped cone of concrete. Care shall be taken\
      \ to prevent vibration of\\n       the samples being tested. The following slumps shall be adopted for different\
      \ kind of\\n       works:\\n\\n     WITH/WITHOUT VIBRATOR\\n        1.   Mass concrete in RCC foundation,\
      \ footings & retaining walls. 10 mm to 25 mm 80\\n       mm\\n        2.   Beams, slabs and columns 25 mm\
      \ to 40 mm 100 mm to 125 mm simply reinforced\\n        3.   Thin RCC section or 40 mm to 50 mm 125 mm to\
      \ 150 mm section with congested\\n              steel\\n\\n8.    PLACING AND COMPACTING:         Method  of\
      \  placing  shall be such as  to\\n       preclude segregation and as far as practicable the placing shall\
      \ be continuous.\\n       Special care shall be taken in accordance with IS 456 while laying concrete under\\\
      n       extreme weather. Concrete, during the operation of placing shall be thoroughly worked\\n       around\
      \ the reinforcements, embedded fixtures, spaded against corners of the formwork\\n      by punning, rodding,\
      \ or by any other approved means and thoroughly compacted by\\n       mechanical vibrators. The number and\
      \ type of vibrator to be used shall be subject to the\\n       approval of the Engineer, and in general immersion\
      \ type vibrators shall be used.\\n\\n9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing\
      \ to the place\\n       of final deposit as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation\
      \ or loss\\n       of any of the ingredients. If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall\
      \ be\\n      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\\n      mixing\
      \ and consolidation in position.\\n\\n10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected\
      \ from damages,  till  it\\n      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall\
      \ be protected\\n      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\\
      n       of any kind and generally fit for drinking.\\n\\n11.  CONSTRUCTION JOINTS:   Construction joints in\
      \ exposed concrete work shall be made\\n      only where shown on the drawings or directed by the Engineer\
      \ and shall be in accordance\\n      with the details shown or approved by the Engineer. The procedure given\
      \ in clause\\n      20.1.4.2 of IS; 456-1978 shall be followed for general guidance. All laitance shall be\\\
      n     removed from the concrete before  it is allowed to fully harden. The removal shall be\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 33 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 33 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\

      \ moisture content of aggregate shall be \\nadded to the cement and aggregates during mixing to produce a\
      \ mixture of sufficient \\nworkability to enable it to be well consolidated, to be worked into the corners\
      \ of the \\nshuttering and around the reinforcements where there is reinforcement to give the \\nspecified\
      \ finish and to have specified strength. \\n \\nNormally for every 50 kg of cement total water including moisture\
      \ content of aggregate \\nshould not be more than 34 liters for 1:3:6 mix 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix. 30 liters\
      \ for 1:1½:3 \\nand 27 liters for 1:1:2. \\n \\nIf difficulty be experienced in placing the concrete of specified\
      \ mix and approved \\nconsistency between and below reinforcement bars, in the bottom of beams and similar\
      \ \\nsituations, the concrete shall have improved workability by increasing the proportion of \\nwater and\
      \ corresponding additional quantity of cement and using aggregates of smaller \\nsize than specified as directed\
      \ by Engineer for which no extra be paid. \\n \\nThe consistency shall be determined by making trial mixtures\
      \ with dried aggregates, or \\nwhen so instructed by test of laboratory made test cubes under the direction\
      \ of Engineer. \\nConsistency may be measured by slump test using a standard cone or the Engineer may \\ndirect\
      \ the use of any other means of testing the consistency. \\n \\nIf the apparatus used for the slump test is\
      \ a standard cone when filled. It shall be raised \\nvertically clear of the concrete and the measurement\
      \ of the slump shall be 300 mm minus \\nthe height of the slumped cone of concrete. Care shall be taken to\
      \ prevent vibration of \\nthe samples being tested. The following slumps shall be adopted for different kind\
      \ of \\nworks: \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        108.00117492675781,\n        364.922607421875,\n\

      : \"WITH/WITHOUT VIBRATOR \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00194549560547,\n        377.7626037597656,\n\
      \        542.2289428710938,\n        628.4780883789062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"1. \\nMass concrete in RCC foundation, footings & retaining walls. 10 mm to 25 mm 80 \\nmm  \\n2. \\nBeams,\
      \ slabs and columns 25 mm to 40 mm 100 mm to 125 mm simply reinforced  \\n3. \\nThin RCC section or 40 mm\
      \ to 50 mm 125 mm to 150 mm section with congested \\nsteel  \\n \\n8. \\nPLACING AND COMPACTING: \\n \\nMethod\
      \ of placing shall be such as to \\npreclude segregation and as far as practicable the placing shall be continuous.\
      \ \\n \\nSpecial care shall be taken in accordance with IS 456 while laying concrete under \\nextreme weather.\
      \ Concrete, during the operation of placing shall be thoroughly worked \\naround the reinforcements, embedded\
      \ fixtures, spaded against corners of the formwork \\nby punning, rodding, or by any other approved means\
      \ and thoroughly compacted by \\nmechanical vibrators. The number and type of vibrator to be used shall be\
      \ subject to the \\napproval of the Engineer, and in general immersion type vibrators shall be used. \\n \\\
      n9.       TRANSPORTING: \\nConcrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place \\nof final deposit\
      \ as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss \\nof any of the ingredients.\
      \ If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be \\nremixed before being placed. Normally\
      \ not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between \\nmixing and consolidation in position. \\n\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00212097167969,\n        630.7225952148438,\n        539.2296752929688,\n\
      \        767.5580444335938\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \" \\n10.  \\n CURING: \\\
      nAll cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages, till it \\nsets and shall be cured thereafter\
      \ for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected \\nfrom drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used\
      \ for curing shall be free from sediments \\nof any kind and generally fit for drinking. \\n \\n11. \\nCONSTRUCTION\
      \ JOINTS: \\nConstruction joints in exposed concrete work shall be made \\nonly where shown on the drawings\
      \ or directed by the Engineer and shall be in accordance \\nwith the details shown or approved by the Engineer.\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\\n      sets\
      \ and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall be protected\\n      from drying\
      \ wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\\n       of any kind and generally\
      \ fit for drinking.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying\
      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n\

      \ shall be protected from damages,  till  it\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 52,\n      \"text\": \"      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. The work shall\
      \ be protected\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"      from drying wind and direct sun rays. Water used for curing shall be free from sediments\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"       of any kind\
      \ and generally fit for drinking.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"source_line_number\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.   REINFORCEMENT:  Steel reinforcement shall be either mild steel of tested quality high\\n        yield\
      \ strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS; 1786--1966, Fe-500 or\\n      as called for on\
      \ the drawings Fabric reinforcement where called for shall be of hard drawn\\n       mild steel wire mesh\
      \ conforming to IS: 1566-1967. Bars shall be free from deleterious\\n        materials, mill scale, loose\
      \ rust, oil or paint. The contractor shall submit bar bending\\n       schedules for approval of the Engineer\
      \ prior to commencement of fabrication. These shall\\n        indicate the accurate dimensions and bending\
      \ of bars as called for on the structural\\n       drawings. Fabrication shall be accurately done to the dimensions,\
      \ spacing and ensuring\\n      minimum cover as called for on structural drawings. All steel shall be rigidly\
      \ held in place\\n       with 18 gauge annealed steel wire. Cement mortar (1;2) blocks of required shape,\
      \ M.S.\\n       chairs and spacer bars shall be used  in order to ensure accurate positioning  of\\n     \
      \  reinforcement.\\n         All reinforcing steel within the limit of a day's polish shall be in place and\
      \ firmly wired at\\n        least one day, prior to the date of pour to permit inspection. The contractor\
      \ shall also\\n       ensure that all conduits and inserts are in position before placing concrete.\\n   \
      \   Hooks where indicated shall be either a complete semi-circular turn with a radius of not\\n        less\
      \ than four and not more than six bar's diameters, plus an extension of at least four\\n       bar diameters\
      \ at the free end, or a 90-degree bend having a radius of not less than 4 bar\\n       diameters plus an extension\
      \ of 12 bar diameters. Unless otherwise shown or specified\\n      on the drawings, all lapped by 50 times\
      \ the diameter of bar. Not more than 33%. (Thirty-\\n      Three Percent) of the bars shall have lapped joints\
      \ at the same location. Only where\\n        specifically shown on the drawings, reinforcement shall have\
      \ welded joints. Rate quoted\\n         for steel reinforcement work shall be deemed to include the cost of\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"2\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"2.\
      \    TESTING OF WORKS AND MATERIALS:              If required by the Engineer/Employer\\n       the Contractor\
      \ shall arrange to test materials, concrete cubes etc. and portions of the\\n      work in order to prove\
      \ their soundness and sufficiency at his own cost. If, after any such\\n        test and in the opinion of\
      \ the Engineer the materials or workmanship is found defective\\n       or unsound, the Contractor shall pull\
      \ down and re-execute the same at his own cost. All\\n       materials deemed defective in the opinion of\
      \ the Engineer shall be removed from the site\\n       immediately.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"\
      2.    TESTING OF WORKS AND MATERIALS:              If required by the Engineer/Employer\",\n  \"retained_lines\"\

- kind: summary
  text: Earthwork specifications cover general requirements in various soils and rocks, including site clearing,
    general filling, compaction, excavation, backfilling, side stabilization, dewatering, access provisions, safety
    measures, lighting, and signage.
  evidence_ids:
  - J6df236b4e4c87756
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C4c6722b90a083638
  citations:
  - id: J6df236b4e4c87756
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B.md
    okf_sha256: 09047352df07521375be4702cc630438bbc82d802fef043c8daf626897b5b9f5
    heading: Clause 17 — Earthwork Including General Filling, Dewatering and Backfilling
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 40
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 1bd9b7794eaf5e192837559f6752a01d367b4a0d6c6764fdf73d30054388100c
    json_pointer: /technical_specifications/16
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"17\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"\
      17.   EARTHWORK INCLUDING GENERAL FILLING, DEWATERING AND BACKFILLING:\\n      These Specifications cover\
      \ the general requirements for earthwork and related works in\\n        different types of soils and rocks.\
      \ The scope of work includes, but is not limited to,\\n        clearing of the site; general filling of site;\
      \ compaction of fill to required degree; excavation\\n      and backfilling works; works related to stabilizing\
      \ of sides of excavations; and necessary\\n       dewatering all within the intent of these specifications.\
      \ The scope shall include provision\\n        of access to different parts of the excavated areas, adequate\
      \ protective safety measures,\\n      and proper lighting and necessary signage arrangements.\\n       General\
      \ filling using approved material shall be done in areas as indicated in drawings\\n        or as directed\
      \ by the Engineer.\\n      The tenderer should acquaint him with the site for assessing the efforts necessary\
      \ for\\n       completing the work as per these specifications.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"17.\
      \   EARTHWORK INCLUDING GENERAL FILLING, DEWATERING AND BACKFILLING:\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"source_line_number\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"17.   EARTHWORK INCLUDING GENERAL\

# Rehabilitation and Restoration Technical Specifications (Vol 2 Part 4B)

> Draft model-derived knowledge. Citation validation establishes supplied-source provenance, not interpretation correctness.

## Summary

- Volume 2 Part 4B establishes technical specifications and execution requirements for rehabilitation and restoration works carried out for safety reasons following the demolition or dismantling of structures under BMRCL Phase 3 Tender Package P1.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `Jf69117de36443ee2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/preamble/0`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33, 34, 35, 36, 37, 38, 39, 40; quotation `S5156636e127c583d`.

- Order of precedence for standards and technical specifications establishes that provisions in the Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical Specifications take priority over General Technical Specifications, KPWD Specifications, MORTH Specifications, and Standard Codes of Practice respectively.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `Je0c9f938cf2098a9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/1`.

- Contractors must obtain all necessary local authority permissions prior to site work, protect existing utilities, and execute electrical and plumbing works exclusively through licensed electrical and plumbing contractors.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `J14c2a5f49d953ca0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/3`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `Ja42deb9b250cc6d2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `Jf1ff2539be6a73c0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/4`.

- Volume 2 Part 4B establishes technical specifications for rehabilitation and restoration works, covering concrete consistency, placement, compaction, transport, curing, joints, strength, formwork, and reinforcement.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 10 — Curing|Clause 10 — Curing]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J2707c9c8171b144c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/9`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 12 — Vertical Joints|Clause 12 — Vertical Joints]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J2fb20bb896254a6b`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/11`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 9 — Transporting|Clause 9 — Transporting]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J508fec85b57149d9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/8`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 7 — Consistency|Clause 7 — Consistency]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 35, 36; quotation `J5bb5caa8190cbcad`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/6`.

JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/13`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 8 — Placing and Compacting|Clause 8 — Placing and Compacting]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `Jc3abe94f6a36730a`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/7`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 11 — Construction Joints|Clause 11 — Construction Joints]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36, 37; quotation `Jcbb4e019d209f8c2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/10`.

- Volume 2 Part 4B establishes technical specifications and execution standards for rehabilitation and restoration works (such as front walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns) required for safety and functional reasons following Building Condition Studies (BCS) under BMRCL Phase 3 Tender Package P1, with measurements restricted to buildings completed under the BCS consultant's supervision.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `J28896027b0708774`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/0`.

- Technical specifications mandate that all materials used in rehabilitation and restoration must be new and of the best quality, complying with Indian Standards, with weigh batching or approved concrete batch mix plants used for concrete production where cement is measured by weight (1 m3 equals 1440 kg or a 50 kg bag equals 35 liters) and coarse aggregate size is specified as 20 mm and down unless otherwise stated.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `Jb5873f13c1466408`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/1`.

- Formwork must be removed without shock or vibration, vertical joints finished against secure temporary shutter stops, and reinforcing steel stored to prevent distortion and corrosion.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J6cd629a1b9456c47`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/other_numbered_lines/6`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 12 — Vertical Joints|Clause 12 — Vertical Joints]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J2fb20bb896254a6b`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/11`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 16 — Stacking and Storage|Clause 16 — Stacking and Storage]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 40; quotation `J0f94d3091220f12b`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/15`.

- Volume 2 Part 4B details the technical specifications and execution standards for concrete works, formwork, reinforcement, and general restoration standards for safety reasons under BMRCL Tender Package P1.

JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/5`.

  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 2 — Testing of Works and Materials|Clause 2 — Testing of Works and Materials]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J698146021cb33654`. - If testing reveals defective or unsound materials or workmanship in the opinion of the Engineer, the Contractor must pull down and re-execute the work at its own cost, and remove defective materials from the site immediately.

## Key Parameters and Source Statements

- BMRCL may appoint a consultant or Engineer to conduct Building Condition Studies (BCS) and recommend remediation, structural restoration (such as front walls, doors, windows, beams, and columns), and demolition sequences, with measurements restricted to buildings completed under such supervision.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `J4d5b89fdb3b2cdb4`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/0`.

- Restoration items executed under this contract follow the KPWD Common Schedule of Rates for the year 2023-24.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `J4d5b89fdb3b2cdb4`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/restoration_instructions/0`.

- Materials used must be new and of the best quality, complying with Indian Standards, with non-availability in metric sizes permitting nearest FPS units upon Engineer approval without extra cost or rebate.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 1 — Materials and Workmanship|Clause 1 — Materials and Workmanship]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33, 34; quotation `J4b8168b15a2bf828`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/0`.

- Samples of materials must be deposited at the Engineer's office for approval at least 15 days before placing supply orders.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 3 — Samples of Material|Clause 3 — Samples of Material]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J6fbd8c1521884f0b`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/2`.

- Storage facilities for materials like cement must be safe, dry, weather-proof with raised wooden flooring, and maintained with at least one month's supply during active construction, arranged in chronological order of receipt.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 4 — Storage|Clause 4 — Storage]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J75650c108b10cc06`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/3`.

- Concrete works require weigh batching (or approved batch mixing plants), mixing times of at least 2 minutes for machine mixing, OPC cement complying with IS 8112 stored in stacks not exceeding 10 bags high, fine aggregates conforming to IS 383 with fineness modulus between 2.60 and 3.20, and coarse aggregates of 20 mm and down size for RCC work unless otherwise stated.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 6 — Cement Concrete Plain and Reinforced|Clause 6 — Cement Concrete Plain and Reinforced]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 35; quotation `J406cfa054c2884b8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/5`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 5 — Specifications for Concrete Works|Clause 5 — Specifications for Concrete Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J73e1bb795fa498c3`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/4`.

- Concrete transport time between mixing and consolidation in position shall normally not exceed 30 minutes.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 9 — Transporting|Clause 9 — Transporting]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J508fec85b57149d9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/8`.

- Cement concrete after laying must be protected from damage until set and cured thereafter for not less than 10 days using water free from sediments and fit for drinking.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 10 — Curing|Clause 10 — Curing]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J2707c9c8171b144c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/9`.

- Formwork must be rigidly constructed using 12 mm thick new plastic coated plywood sheets for columns, pedestals, beams, walls, and fascia, and steel or plywood for roof slabs, canopies, floors, cover slabs, and retaining walls.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering|Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37, 38, 39; quotation `J920a9f596916d4bf`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/13`.

- Minimum cement quantities for concrete grades are specified as: 3.40 bags/cum for 1:4:8, 4.40 bags/cum for 1:3:6, 6.40 bags/cum for 1:2:4, 8.00 bags/cum for 1:1.5:3 plain and RCC, and 10.80 bags/cum for RCC 1:1:2.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering|Clause 14 — Concrete Formwork and Centering]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37, 38, 39; quotation `J920a9f596916d4bf`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/13`.

- Steel reinforcement shall consist of mild steel of tested quality or high yield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS:1786-1966, Fe-500, or as specified on drawings, with laps generally equal to 50 times the bar diameter and not more than 33% of bars lapped at the same location.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 15 — Reinforcement|Clause 15 — Reinforcement]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 40; quotation `J6b4d4ddf8645b289`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/14`.

- The order of precedence for rehabilitation and restoration standards and technical specifications gives top priority to provisions in the Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & Technical Specifications, followed by Technical Specifications, KPWD Specifications, MORTH Specifications, and Standard Codes of Practice respectively.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 33; quotation `J28896027b0708774`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/0`.

- Materials samples must be deposited at the Engineer's office for approval at least 15 days before placing supply orders, and cement storage must be maintained adequate for at least 1 month during active construction.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `Jb5873f13c1466408`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/1`.

- Fresh concrete must be conveyed from mixing to final deposit within not more than 30 minutes to prevent segregation, protected against drying wind and direct sun, and cured for not less than 10 days.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 9 — Transporting|Clause 9 — Transporting]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J508fec85b57149d9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/8`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 10 — Curing|Clause 10 — Curing]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J2707c9c8171b144c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/9`.

- Minimum cement contents for concrete grades are specified as follows: CC 1:4:8 at 3.40 bags/cum, CC 1:3:6 at 4.40 bags/cum, CC 1:2:4 at 6.40 bags/cum, CC 1:1.5:3 at 8.00 bags/cum, RCC 1:1.5:3 at 8.00 bags/cum, and RCC 1:1:2 at 10.80 bags/cum.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 38; quotation `J0fa404cc10eb35ce`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/5`.

- Maximum total water including aggregate moisture per 50 kg of cement is restricted to 34 liters for 1:3:6 mix, 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix, 30 liters for 1:1.5:3 mix, and 27 liters for 1:1:2 mix.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works|Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4B: Specifications for Rehabilitation and Restoration Works]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J47015c69706b0a82`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/source_page_context/3`.

- Cement concrete after laying must be protected from damage and cured for not less than 10 days using clean water free from sediments.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 10 — Curing|Clause 10 — Curing]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 36; quotation `J2707c9c8171b144c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/9`.

- Steel reinforcement comprises high yield strength deformed bars of grade Fe-415 conforming to IS 1786-1966, Fe-500, or mild steel, with lap lengths specified as 50 times the bar diameter unless otherwise shown.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 15 — Reinforcement|Clause 15 — Reinforcement]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 40; quotation `J6b4d4ddf8645b289`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/14`.

- General filling must use approved material in areas indicated in drawings or directed by the Engineer, and tenderers must acquaint themselves with the site to assess effort.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 17 — Earthwork Including General Filling, Dewatering and Backfilling|Clause 17 — Earthwork Including General Filling, Dewatering and Backfilling]]; `Vol2DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 40; quotation `J6df236b4e4c87756`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol2/part_4b_restoration/restoration_clauses.json#/technical_specifications/16`.

> This part contains the specifications for rehabilitation and restoration works (Part 4, Part B) of Section H in Volume 2 of BMRCL Tender No. `BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157`, concerning restoration works carried out for safety reasons after demolition or dismantling of buildings and structures identified for Bangalore Metro Rail Project Phase 3.

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 1 — Materials and Workmanship|Clause 1 — Materials and Workmanship]]

>       "pdf_page": 33,
>       "text": "1.    MATERIALS AND WORKMANSHIP:     The whole of the materials used on the work\n               shall be new and of the best quality of their respective kind. Other articles, tools, plants\n               shall be the best of the several kinds available in the market and shall be used in the best,\n           most workman like, and most substantial manner to the complete satisfaction of the\n             Engineer.\n           The workmanship is to be the best available and of a high standard. The Contractor shall\n             take all steps immediately to make up deficiency if any noticed by the Engineer. Use must\n           be made of a special tradesman in all aspects of the work and allowances must be made\n                in the rates for the same.\n\n              Contractor shall maintain uniform quality and consistency in workmanship throughout the\n             execution of the work."

### J75650c108b10cc06

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 4 — Storage|Clause 4 — Storage]]

>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 34,
>       "text": "4.    STORAGE:   Safe, dry and proper storage shall be provided for all materials, particularly\n        for materials like cement. Adequate storage should be made at least sufficient for 1 month\n       during active construction.\n      The Contractor shall procure necessary guarantees from the manufactures and furnish\n       the same to the Engineer."

### J406cfa054c2884b8

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 6 — Cement Concrete Plain and Reinforced|Clause 6 — Cement Concrete Plain and Reinforced]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "6",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 35,
>       "text": "6.   CEMENT CONCRETE PLAIN AND REINFORCED:\n\n            A) Cement:  Cement shall comply in every respect with the requirements of the latest\n                 publication of IS: 8112 and unless otherwise specified, ordinary Portland cement shall\n             be used. No other make of cement but that approved by the Engineer will be allowed\n             on works and the source of supply not be changed without approval of the Engineer in\n                   writing. Test certificates to show that the cement used fully complies with the relevant\n                IS specifications shall be submitted to the Engineer and not withstanding this, the\n               Engineer may at their discretion order that the cement brought to site and which they\n            may consider damaged or of doubtful quality for any reasons whatsoever shall be re-\n                tested in an approved testing laboratory and fresh certificate of its soundness shall be\n               produced. Cement ordered for retesting shall not be used for any work pending results\n                  of retest.\n             Cement shall be stored and neatly packed in piles not exceeding 10 bags high in\n               weather proof sheds with raised wooden plank flooring to prevent deterioration by\n             dampness or intrusion of foreign matter. It shall be stored in such a way as to allow\n                the removal and use of cement in chronological order of receipt, i.e. the first received\n               being first used. Cement deteriorated and/or clotted shall not be used on work but shall\n             be removed at once from the site.\n\n           B) Fine Aggregates: Sand shall conform to IS: 383. It shall pass through 1.5 sieve 4.75\n         mm (3/16 B.S) test sieve leaving a residue not more than 5%. It shall be from a natural\n               source or crushed stone screedings.  It shall be washed  if directed to reduce the\n               percentage of deleterious substances to acceptable limits. Sand shall not contain any\n                 trace of salt and sand containing any trace of salt shall be rejected.\n             The fine aggregate for concrete shall be graded within limits as specified in IS: 383\n             and the fineness modules shall range between 2.60 to 3.20. The fine aggregate shall\n               stacked carefully on a clean hard dry surface so that  it will not get mixed up with\n                 deleterious foreign materials. If such a surface is not available a platform of planks or\n                corrugated sheets or brick floor or concrete floor shall be prepared. Sand shall be\n             added in the desired proportion as required for the strength specified, with suitable\n                 correction for bulking.\n\n           C) Coarse Aggregates:    Coarse aggregate shall conform to IS: 383. It shall consist\n                  of crushed or broken stone, 95% of which shall, be retained on 4.75 mm is test sieve.\n                          It shall be obtained from crushed granite, trap, basalt or similar approved stones from\n              approved quarry. Coarse aggregate shall be chemically inert when mixed with cement\n           & shall be angular in shape and free from soft friable thin porous laminated or flaky\n                pieces. It shall be free from dust and other foreign matter. Gravel/shingle of desired\n                grading may be permitted as a substitute in part or full in plain cement concrete if the\n               Engineer are otherwise satisfied about the quality of aggregate.\n\n           D) Water:    Water used for mixing concrete shall be fresh, clean, free from oil, salts,\n                 acids, alkali and shall be in accordance with the clause 4.3 of IS 456 - 1978. The\n                 contractor shall produce test results for approval by the Engineer for the mixing of\n               water proposed to be used on the job, before casting any concrete.\n\n            E) Mixing Of Concrete:    MACHINE  MIXED:  Aggregates  shall  be  accurately\n             measured out in boxes and mixed dry along with required cement, water shall then be\n             added in measured quantity and mixing shall be continued until there is uniform\n                  distribution of the materials and the mass is uniform in color and consistency but in no\n              case shall the mixing be done for less than 2 minutes. Only hopper loading mixer shall\n             be used."
>     }
>   ],

### J73e1bb795fa498c3

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 5 — Specifications for Concrete Works|Clause 5 — Specifications for Concrete Works]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "5",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 34,
>       "text": "5.    SPECIFICATIONS FOR CONCRETE WORKS\n\n      Concrete General\n      Wherever grade of concrete like M15, M20 etc. are mentioned, the concrete mix shall be\n       designed by the contractor and approval of Engineer for the same shall be obtained.\n      Weigh batching shall be adopted for producing the concrete. Obtaining of concrete from\n       concrete batch mix plant will be permitted. Contractor shall obtain approval of Engineer\n         for each such batching plant. Proportion of ordinary cement concrete will be expressed\n      as l:3:6, 1:2:4, 1:1.5:3 etc. in the specification. The first figure will be the quantity of\n      cement by volume, the second figure will be dry coarse sand (fine aggregate) by volume\n      and the third figure will be the quantity of course aggregate by volume. Cement shall be\n      measured by weight. The weight is to be derived on the basis that 1 cubic meter of\n      cement will weigh 1440 kgs., or one full bag of 50 kgs. will be assumed to be 35 ltrs.\n      when the sand is wet or moist suitable correction for bulking is to be given while\n        proportioning. Engineer may allow measuring cement by volume.\n       Unless otherwise specified the rates for all RCC will be exclusive or reinforcements.\n       Reinforcements will be paid for separate1y.\n       Unless, otherwise stated, for all RCC work the size of course aggregate will be 20 mm\n      and down size."
>     }
>   ],

### J2707c9c8171b144c

>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 36,
>       "text": "10.   CURING:      All cement concrete after laying shall be protected from damages,  till  it\n      sets and shall be cured thereafter for not less than 10 days. ### J508fec85b57149d9

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 9 — Transporting|Clause 9 — Transporting]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "9",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 36,
>       "text": "9.   TRANSPORTING:    Concrete shall be conveyed from the place of mixing to the place\n       of final deposit as rapidly as practicable by methods which will prevent segregation or loss\n       of any of the ingredients. If segregation does occur during transport, the concrete shall be\n      remixed before being placed. Normally not more than 30 minutes shall lapse between\n      mixing and consolidation in position."
>     }

### J5bb5caa8190cbcad

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4B#Clause 7 — Consistency|Clause 7 — Consistency]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "7",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 35,
>       "text": "7.  CONSISTENCY:"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 36,
>       "text": "Only sufficient water giving due allowance for the moisture content of aggregate shall be\n      added to the cement and aggregates during mixing to produce a mixture of sufficient\n        workability to enable  it to be well consolidated, to be worked into the corners of the\n       shuttering and around the reinforcements where there is reinforcement to give the\n       specified finish and to have specified strength.\n       Normally for every 50 kg of cement total water including moisture content of aggregate\n       should not be more than 34 liters for 1:3:6 mix 32 liters for 1:2:4 mix. 30 liters for 1:1½:3\n      and 27 liters for 1:1:2.\n            If  difficulty be experienced in placing the concrete of specified mix and approved\n       consistency between and below reinforcement bars, in the bottom of beams and similar\n        situations, the concrete shall have improved workability by increasing the proportion of\n       water and corresponding additional quantity of cement and using aggregates of smaller\n        size than specified as directed by Engineer for which no extra be paid.\n      The consistency shall be determined by making trial mixtures with dried aggregates, or\n      when so instructed by test of laboratory made test cubes under the direction of Engineer.\n       Consistency may be measured by slump test using a standard cone or the Engineer may\n        direct the use of any other means of testing the consistency.\n            If the apparatus used for the slump test is a standard cone when filled. It shall be raised\n         vertically clear of the concrete and the measurement of the slump shall be 300 mm minus\n       the height of the slumped cone of concrete. Care shall be taken to prevent vibration of\n       the samples being tested. The following slumps shall be adopted for different kind of\n       works:\n\n     WITH/WITHOUT VIBRATOR\n        1.   Mass concrete in RCC foundation, footings & retaining walls. 10 mm to 25 mm 80\n       mm\n        2.   Beams, slabs and columns 25 mm to 40 mm 100 mm to 125 mm simply reinforced\n        3.   Thin RCC section or 40 mm to 50 mm 125 mm to 150 mm section with congested\n              steel"
>     }
>   ],
